83	  return x;
84	};
85	interface HeadX { f2: number; f3: number; l0: number; l1: number; l2: number }
86	const headX = new WeakMap<Enemy, HeadX>();
87	const headOf = (e: Enemy): HeadX => {
88	  let x = headX.get(e);
89	  if (!x) { x = { f2: 0, f3: 0, l0: 0, l1: 0, l2: 0 }; headX.set(e, x); }
90	  return x;
91	};
92	
93	/** 实心块内判定(原版 Collision.SolidCollision 的中心点近似) */
94	const inSolid = (game: GameHooks, e: Enemy): boolean => {
95	  const st = game.world.store;
96	  const tx = Math.floor(e.cx / TILE), ty = Math.floor(e.cy / TILE);
97	  return st.inBounds(tx, ty) && st.isSolid(tx, ty);
98	};
99	
100	/** 原版"攻击时间表"推进(NPC.cs :37229-37251 / :37740-37762 / :38243-38264 同构):
101	 *  ai1 全局计时,逐槽累计时长定位当前槽;全部槽走完回绕 ai1=0。
102	 *  注意:只回写 ai1,不写 ai0——手/头由调用方把槽攻击号赋给 ai0(即其状态),
103	 *  核心二阶段 ai0 是相位状态(0/1/2/3),绝不能被攻击号覆盖。 */
104	function attackSlot(e: Enemy, table: ReadonlyArray<readonly [number, number]>): { atk: number; inSlot: number; dur: number } {
105	  let slot = 0, consumed = 0;
106	  while (slot < table.length) {
107	    const dur = table[slot][1];
108	    if (dur + consumed > e.ai1) break;
109	    consumed += dur;
110	    slot++;
111	  }
112	  if (slot === table.length) { slot = 0; e.ai1 = 0; consumed = 0; }
113	  const atk = table[slot][0];
114	  return { atk, inSlot: e.ai1 - consumed, dur: table[slot][1] };
115	}
116	
117	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
118	// AI_069 猪鲨鱼龙(NPC 370,NPC.cs:49143-50127)
119	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
120	// 状态 ai0:-1 出生(75t) → 一阶段循环 0 悬浮(num3 t)→1 冲撞(num6 t)×5→2 泡泡弹(80t)
121	//   →3 鲨鱼龙卷(90t);血 ≤50% →4 变身(180t) →5 二阶段循环:0→6 冲撞→7 旋转泡泡冲刺(120t)
122	//   →8 鲨鱼龙卷;9(expert 15% 血)二段变身(180t) →10 隐身悬浮→11 冲撞/12 瞬移(30t)/13 旋转冲刺。
123	// 经典参数:悬浮 60t/0.45/7.5、冲撞 30t@16 速、泡泡 80t 每 4t 一发、龙卷 90t(60t 时发射)、变身 180t。
124	
125	const DUKE = {
126	  spawnT: 75,          // num21 :49215
127	  hoverT: 60,          // num3  :49168(经典)
128	  hoverAcc: 0.45,      // num4  :49169
129	  hoverSpd: 7.5,       // num5  :49170
130	  chargeT: 30,         // num6  :49187
131	  chargeSpd: 16,       // num7  :49188
132	  bubbleT: 80,         // num8  :49202
133	  bubbleEvery: 4,      // num9  :49203
134	  bubbleAcc: 0.3,      // num10 :49204
135	  bubbleSpd: 5,        // num11 :49205
136	  nadoT: 90,           // num12 :49206
137	  p1T: 180,            // num13 :49207(一→二阶段变身)
138	  p2T: 180,            // num14 :49208(二段狂暴变身,expert)
139	  teleT: 30,           // num15 :49209(瞬移)
140	  spinT: 120,          // num16 :49210(旋转冲刺)
141	  spinEvery: 4,        // num17 :49211
142	  spinBubbleSpd: 6,    // num18 :49212
143	  spinDashSpd: 20,     // num19 :49213
144	};
145	/** 旋转冲刺角速度 num20 = 2π/(num16/2) = 2π/60(:49214) */
146	const DUKE_SPIN_RATE = TAU / (DUKE.spinT / 2);
147	
148	/** 狂暴判定 flag6(:49238):玩家在太空(y<800)/地下(y>worldSurface*16)/离开两侧海洋带 */
149	function dukeEnraged(game: GameHooks, player: Player): boolean {
150	  const st = game.world.store;
151	  const surface = (game.world.groundLevel || Math.floor(st.h * 0.3)) * TILE;
152	  return player.y < 800 || player.y > surface ||
153	    (player.x > 6400 && player.x < st.w * TILE - 6400);
154	}
155	
156	/** 一阶段悬浮结束的下一招(:49471-49542) */
157	function dukePick1(e: Enemy, enraged: boolean): number {
158	  const a3 = Math.floor(e.ai3);
159	  let next = 0; // 0=无事(原版 switch 无匹配分支,ai2 继续累计)
160	  if (a3 >= 0 && a3 <= 9) next = 1;          // 冲撞
161	  else if (a3 === 10) { e.ai3 = 1; next = 2; } // 泡泡弹
162	  else if (a3 === 11) { e.ai3 = 0; next = 3; } // 鲨鱼龙卷
163	  if (enraged && next === 2) next = 3;       // :49495 狂暴时泡泡弹换龙卷
164	  if (e.hp <= e.maxHp * 0.5) next = 4;       // :49499 半血变身
165	  return next;
166	}
167	/** 二阶段悬浮结束的下一招(:49736-49809) */
168	function dukePick2(e: Enemy, enraged: boolean): number {
169	  const a3 = Math.floor(e.ai3);
170	  let next = 0;
171	  if (a3 >= 0 && a3 <= 5) next = 1;          // 冲撞(6)
172	  else if (a3 === 6) { e.ai3 = 1; next = 2; } // 旋转泡泡冲刺(7)
173	  else if (a3 === 7) { e.ai3 = 0; next = 3; } // 鲨鱼龙卷(8)
174	  // flag2(expert hp≤15%):next=4 → ai0=9(:49756-49759)——经典模式不可达,代码保留见 dukeState9
175	  if (enraged && next === 2) next = 3;       // :49760
176	  return next;
177	}
178	/** 冲撞起手(:49503-49520 / :49764-49781 / :49988-50004 同构):定速直冲+朝向修正 */
179	function dukeCharge(e: Enemy, player: Player, spd: number, toState: number, st: DukeX) {
180	  const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
181	  const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
182	  e.vx = (dx / d) * spd;
183	  e.vy = (dy / d) * spd;
184	  e.ai0 = toState; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0;
185	  e.spin = Math.atan2(e.vy, e.vx);
186	  const s = Math.sign(dx) || st.dir;
187	  st.dir = s;
188	  if (st.spr === 1) e.spin += Math.PI;
189	  st.spr = -s;
190	}
191	/** 悬浮段共用(:49415-49468 / :49680-49733 / :49932-49967 同构):
192	 *  目标点 = 玩家 + (ai1 侧偏, -200);desired = normalize(目标-自身-速度)*spd 后 SimpleFlyMovement */
193	function dukeHover(e: Enemy, player: Player, acc: number, spd: number, sideOff: number, st: DukeX) {
194	  if (e.ai1 === 0) e.ai1 = sideOff * Math.sign(e.cx - player.cx || 1);
195	  const dx = player.cx + e.ai1 - e.cx - e.vx;
196	  const dy = player.cy - 200 - e.cy - e.vy;
197	  const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
198	  e.vx = accelTo(e.vx, (dx / d) * spd, acc);
199	  e.vy = accelTo(e.vy, (dy / d) * spd, acc);
200	  const s = Math.sign(player.cx - e.cx) || st.dir;
201	  if (e.ai2 === 0 && s !== st.dir) e.spin += Math.PI;
202	  st.dir = s;
203	  if (st.spr !== -st.dir) e.spin += Math.PI;
204	  st.spr = -st.dir;
205	}
206	
207	/** AI_069 主入口(经典模式) */
208	export function dukeFishronAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
209	  const st = dukeOf(e);
210	  if (!e.bInit) { e.bInit = true; e.ai0 = -1; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 = 0; e.spawnAlpha = 255; } // :49247-49257
211	  const b = baseOf(e);
212	  const enraged = player ? dukeEnraged(game, player) : false;
213	  const flag = e.hp <= e.maxHp * 0.5;                       // flag  :49147 二阶段
214	  const flag3 = e.ai0 > 4;                                  // flag3 :49149(二阶段状态 5-13)
215	  const flag4 = e.ai0 > 9;                                  // flag4 :49150(expert 狂暴段 10-13)
216	  const flag5 = e.ai3 < (flag3 ? 3 : 5) * 2;                // flag5 :49151-49152
217	
218	  // 伤害/防御修正(:49153-49167 + 狂暴 :49239-49245)——每 tick 从基值派生
219	  if (flag4) { e.def.damage = Math.round(b.dmg * 1.1); e.def.defense = 0; }
220	  else if (flag3) { e.def.damage = Math.round(b.dmg * 1.2); e.def.defense = Math.round(b.def * 0.8); }
221	  else { e.def.damage = b.dmg; e.def.defense = b.def; }
222	  if (enraged) { e.def.damage = b.dmg * 2; e.def.defense = b.def * 2; }
223	
224	  // 悬浮/冲撞参数(:49168-49201;expert 档注释)
225	  let hoverT = DUKE.hoverT, hoverAcc = DUKE.hoverAcc, hoverSpd = DUKE.hoverSpd;
226	  if (flag4) { hoverAcc = 0.7; hoverSpd = 12; hoverT = 30; }            // :49171-49176
227	  else if (flag3 && flag5) { hoverAcc = 0.5; hoverSpd = 8; hoverT = 20; } // :49177-49182
228	  else if (flag5 && !flag3 && !flag4) hoverT = 30;                       // :49183-49186
229	  let chargeT = DUKE.chargeT, chargeSpd = DUKE.chargeSpd;
230	  if (flag4) { chargeT = 25; chargeSpd = 27; }                           // :49189-49193(expert 同档)
231	  if (enraged) { hoverT = 10; chargeSpd += 6; }                          // :49239-49245
232	
233	  // 玩家死/远离 5600px(:49218-49237):减速升空离场(原版另 EncourageDespawn(10);
234	  // 本工程 Boss 不参与 despawn 块,故仅保留升空)。原版此处无回血逻辑。
235	  if (!player || player.dead || Math.hypot(player.cx - e.cx, player.cy - e.cy) > 5600) {
236	    e.vy -= 0.4;
237	    e.encourageDespawn(10); // :49234 急升逃离 + EncourageDespawn(10)
238	    e.ai0 = e.ai0 > 4 ? 5 : 0;
239	    e.ai2 = 0;
240	    e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy;
241	    return;
242	  }
243	
244	  // ---- 身体旋转朝向(:49258-49345)----
245	  let target = Math.atan2(player.cy - e.cy, player.cx - e.cx);
246	  if (st.spr === 1) target += Math.PI;
247	  if (target < 0) target += TAU; else if (target > TAU) target -= TAU;
248	  if (e.ai0 === -1 || e.ai0 === 3 || e.ai0 === 4 || e.ai0 === 8) target = 0; // :49271-49286
249	  let step = 0.04;                                                            // num23 :49287
250	  if (e.ai0 === 1 || e.ai0 === 6 || e.ai0 === 7) step = 0;                    // :49288-49295
251	  if (e.ai0 === 3 || e.ai0 === 4 || e.ai0 === 8) step = 0.01;                 // :49296-49307
252	  let rot = e.spin;
253	  if (rot < target) rot += (target - rot > Math.PI ? -step : step);
254	  if (rot > target) rot += (rot - target > Math.PI ? step : -step);
255	  if (rot > target - step && rot < target + step) rot = target;
256	  if (rot < 0) rot += TAU; else if (rot > TAU) rot -= TAU;
257	  if (rot > target - step && rot < target + step) rot = target;
258	  e.spin = rot;
259	
260	  // ---- 透明度(:49346-49363;近似为 spawnAlpha 渐变)----
261	  // 入实心块 +15/出块 −15,钳 [0,150](★上限 150 非 255——入块半隐,2026-08-19 台账 E 修)
262	  if (e.ai0 !== -1 && e.ai0 < 9) e.spawnAlpha = inSolid(game, e) ? Math.min(150, e.spawnAlpha + 15) : Math.max(0, e.spawnAlpha - 15);
263	
264	  let invuln = false; // flag7(dontTakeDamage = !flag7,:50126)
265	  switch (e.ai0) {
266	    case -1: { // 出生(:49365-49414)
267	      invuln = true;
268	      e.vx *= 0.98; e.vy *= 0.98;
269	      const s = Math.sign(player.cx - e.cx) || st.dir;
270	      st.dir = s; st.spr = -s;
271	      if (e.ai2 > 20) {
272	        e.vy = -2;
273	        e.spawnAlpha = clamp(e.spawnAlpha - 5 + (inSolid(game, e) ? 15 : 0), 0, 150); // :49375-49391
274	      } else if (e.spawnAlpha < 255) e.spawnAlpha = 255;
275	      if (e.ai2 === DUKE.nadoT - 30) { // :49392 出生尘环
276	        game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, '#8FD8FF', 18, 2.4, { life: 40 });
277	        game.playSfxWav?.('Zombie_20', 1, e.cx, e.cy);   // :49404 PlaySound(29,…,20) 满响(曾借 roar 0.8)
278	      }
279	      e.ai2 += 1;
280	      if (e.ai2 >= DUKE.spawnT) { e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; }
281	      break;
282	    }
283	    case 0: { // 一阶段悬浮(:49415-49543)
284	      if (player.dead) break;
285	      dukeHover(e, player, hoverAcc, hoverSpd, 300, st);
286	      e.ai2 += 1;
287	      if (e.ai2 >= hoverT) {
288	        const next = dukePick1(e, enraged);
289	        if (next === 1) dukeCharge(e, player, chargeSpd, 1, st);
290	        else if (next === 2) { e.ai0 = 2; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; }
291	        else if (next === 3) {
292	          e.ai0 = 3; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0;
293	          if (enraged) e.ai2 = DUKE.nadoT - 40; // :49530-49533 狂暴跳过前摇
294	        } else if (next === 4) { e.ai0 = 4; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; }
295	      }
296	      break;
297	    }
298	    case 1: { // 冲撞(:49544-49566)
299	      game.spawnParticles(e.cx + e.w / 2 - Math.cos(e.spin) * e.w * 0.3, e.cy + e.h / 2 - Math.sin(e.spin) * e.h * 0.3, '#8FD8FF', 2, 1.5, { life: 18 });
300	      e.ai2 += 1;
301	      if (e.ai2 >= chargeT) { e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 += 2; }
302	      break;
303	    }
304	    case 2: { // 泡泡弹幕(:49567-49637)
305	      if (player.dead) break;
306	      if (e.ai1 === 0) e.ai1 = 300 * Math.sign(e.cx - player.cx || 1);
307	      const dx = player.cx + e.ai1 - e.cx - e.vx;
308	      const dy = player.cy - 200 - e.cy - e.vy;
309	      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
310	      e.vx = accelTo(e.vx, (dx / d) * DUKE.bubbleSpd, DUKE.bubbleAcc);
311	      e.vy = accelTo(e.vy, (dy / d) * DUKE.bubbleSpd, DUKE.bubbleAcc);
312	      if (e.ai2 === 0) game.playSfxWav?.('Zombie_20', 1, e.cx, e.cy);   // :49608 PlaySound(29,…,20) 满响(曾借 roar 0.5)
313	      if (e.ai2 % DUKE.bubbleEvery === 0) { // :49610-49618 每 4t 吐一颗爆裂泡泡(NPC 371)
314	        const ddx = player.cx - e.cx, ddy = player.cy - e.cy;
315	        const dd = Math.hypot(ddx, ddy) || 1;
316	        const bub = spawnPart(game, e, 371);
317	        if (bub) {
318	          bub.x = e.cx + (ddx / dd) * ((e.w + 20) / 2) - bub.w / 2;
319	          bub.y = e.cy + (ddy / dd) * ((e.w + 20) / 2) + 45 - bub.h / 2;
320	        }
321	        // :49612 每吐一颗播 PlaySound(4,…,19)=NPC_Killed_19(case4=死音库)
322	        game.playSfxWav?.('NPC_Killed_19', 1, e.cx, e.cy);
323	      }
324	      const s = Math.sign(player.cx - e.cx) || st.dir;
325	      st.dir = s; st.spr = -s;
326	      e.ai2 += 1;
327	      if (e.ai2 >= DUKE.bubbleT) { e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; }
328	      break;
329	    }
330	    case 3: dukeSharknado(e, game, player, st, false); break;          // :49638-49660
331	    case 4: { // 一→二阶段变身(:49661-49679)
332	      invuln = true;
333	      e.vx *= 0.98;
334	      e.vy = lerp(e.vy, 0, 0.02);
335	      if (e.ai2 === DUKE.p1T - 60) game.playSfxWav?.('Zombie_20', 1, e.cx, e.cy);   // :49668 PlaySound(29,…,20)(曾借 roar 0.9)
336	      e.ai2 += 1;
337	      if (e.ai2 >= DUKE.p1T) { e.ai0 = 5; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 = 0; }
338	      break;
339	    }
340	    case 5: { // 二阶段悬浮(:49680-49811)
341	      if (player.dead) break;
342	      dukeHover(e, player, hoverAcc, hoverSpd, 300, st);
343	      e.ai2 += 1;
344	      if (e.ai2 >= hoverT) {
345	        const next = dukePick2(e, enraged);
346	        if (next === 1) dukeCharge(e, player, chargeSpd, 6, st);
347	        else if (next === 2) { // 旋转泡泡冲刺起手(:49782-49797):20 速直冲
348	          const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
349	          const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
350	          e.vx = (dx / d) * DUKE.spinDashSpd;
351	          e.vy = (dy / d) * DUKE.spinDashSpd;
352	          e.spin = Math.atan2(e.vy, e.vx);
353	          const s = Math.sign(dx) || st.dir;
354	          st.dir = s;
355	          if (st.spr === 1) e.spin += Math.PI;
356	          st.spr = -s;
357	          e.ai0 = 7; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0;
358	        } else if (next === 3) { e.ai0 = 8; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; }
359	        else if (next === 4) { e.ai0 = 9; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; } // expert
360	      }
361	      break;
362	    }
363	    case 6: { // 二阶段冲撞(:49812-49834)
364	      game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, '#8FD8FF', 2, 1.5, { life: 18 });
365	      e.ai2 += 1;
366	      if (e.ai2 >= chargeT) { e.ai0 = 5; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 += 2; }
367	      break;
368	    }
369	    case 7: { // 旋转泡泡冲刺(:49835-49864)
370	      if (e.ai2 === 0) game.playSfxWav?.('Zombie_20', 1, e.cx, e.cy);   // :49839 PlaySound(29,…,20)(曾借 roar 0.6)
371	      if (e.ai2 % DUKE.spinEvery === 0) { // 每 4t 沿垂直方向甩一颗泡泡
372	        const vlen = Math.hypot(e.vx, e.vy) || 1;
373	        const bub = spawnPart(game, e, 371);
374	        game.playSfxWav?.('NPC_Killed_19', 1, e.cx, e.cy);   // :49843 每颗吐音(case4,19)
375	        if (bub) {
376	          bub.bInit = true; bub.ai0 = 0; bub.ai1 = 0;          // 预置初速,跳过自初始化
377	          bub.ai3 = (80 + Math.random() * 41) / 100;            // :49751 随机 scale
378	          bub.x = e.cx + (e.vx / vlen) * ((e.w + 20) / 2) - bub.w / 2;
379	          bub.y = e.cy + (e.vy / vlen) * ((e.w + 20) / 2) + 45 - bub.h / 2;
380	          const ang = Math.atan2(e.vy / vlen, e.vx / vlen) + (Math.PI / 2) * st.dir;
381	          bub.vx = Math.cos(ang) * DUKE.spinBubbleSpd;
382	          bub.vy = Math.sin(ang) * DUKE.spinBubbleSpd;
383	        }
384	      }
385	      // 速度/身体同步旋转(-num20*direction,:49854-49855)
386	      {
387	        const r = -DUKE_SPIN_RATE * st.dir;
388	        const cos = Math.cos(r), sin = Math.sin(r);
389	        const nvx = e.vx * cos - e.vy * sin, nvy = e.vx * sin + e.vy * cos;
390	        e.vx = nvx; e.vy = nvy;
391	        e.spin -= DUKE_SPIN_RATE * st.dir;
392	      }
393	      e.ai2 += 1;
394	      if (e.ai2 >= DUKE.spinT) { e.ai0 = 5; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; }
395	      break;
396	    }
397	    case 8: dukeSharknado(e, game, player, st, true); break;           // :49865-49885
398	    case 9: { // expert 二段狂暴变身(:49886-49931;经典不可达,保留供 expert 接线)
399	      invuln = true;
400	      if (e.ai2 < DUKE.p2T - 90) e.spawnAlpha = clamp(e.spawnAlpha + (inSolid(game, e) ? 15 : -15), 0, 255);
401	      else e.spawnAlpha = Math.min(255, e.spawnAlpha + 4);
402	      e.vx *= 0.98;
403	      e.vy = lerp(e.vy, 0, 0.02);
404	      if (e.ai2 === DUKE.p2T - 60) game.playSfxWav?.('Zombie_20', 1, e.cx, e.cy);   // :49920 PlaySound(29,…,20)(曾借 roar 0.9)
405	      e.ai2 += 1;
406	      if (e.ai2 >= DUKE.p2T) { e.ai0 = 10; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 = 0; }
407	      break;
408	    }
409	    case 10: { // expert 狂暴段悬浮(:49932-50018)
410	      if (player.dead) break;
411	      e.spawnAlpha = Math.min(255, e.spawnAlpha + 25); // 隐身逼近
412	      dukeHover(e, player, hoverAcc, hoverSpd, 360, st);
413	      e.ai2 += 1;
414	      if (e.ai2 >= hoverT) {
415	        const a3 = Math.floor(e.ai3);
416	        const next = (a3 === 0 || a3 === 2 || a3 === 3 || a3 === 5 || a3 === 6 || a3 === 7) ? 1 : 2;
417	        if (next === 1) dukeCharge(e, player, chargeSpd, 11, st);
418	        else { e.ai0 = 12; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; }
419	      }
420	      break;
421	    }
422	    case 11: { // expert 狂暴段冲撞(:50019-50047)
423	      e.spawnAlpha = Math.max(0, e.spawnAlpha - 25);
424	      game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, '#8FD8FF', 2, 1.5, { life: 18 });
425	      e.ai2 += 1;
426	      if (e.ai2 >= chargeT) { e.ai0 = 10; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 += 1; }
427	      break;
428	    }
429	    case 12: { // expert 瞬移爆冲(:50048-50107)
430	      invuln = true;
431	      e.spawnAlpha = Math.min(255, e.spawnAlpha + 17);
432	      e.vx *= 0.98;
433	      e.vy = lerp(e.vy, 0, 0.02);
434	      if (e.ai2 === DUKE.teleT / 2) { // :50066-50093 中点瞬移到玩家另一侧
435	        if (e.ai1 === 0) e.ai1 = 300 * Math.sign(e.cx - player.cx || 1);
436	        e.x = player.cx - e.ai1 - e.w / 2;
437	        e.y = player.cy - 200 - e.h / 2;
438	        // :50075-50091 侧翻补偿:瞬移后 num41=玩家在新身哪侧,direction 重定;
439	        // spriteDirection != -direction 时 rotation += π(朝向翻转 180°,
440	        // 2026-08-19 台账 E 补——曾漏 = 瞬移后鱼头朝向不变);(原版 :50078 的
441	        // ai[2]==0 门在 ai[2]==num15/2 内恒假=死代码,略)
442	        const s = Math.sign(player.cx - e.cx);
443	        if (s !== 0) {
444	          st.dir = s;
445	          if (st.spr !== -s) e.spin += Math.PI;
446	          st.spr = -s;
447	        }
448	        game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, '#8FD8FF', 14, 2.4, { life: 30 });
449	        game.playSfxWav?.('Zombie_20', 1, e.cx, e.cy);   // :50064 PlaySound(29,…,20)(曾借 roar 0.6)
450	      }
451	      e.ai2 += 1;
452	      if (e.ai2 >= DUKE.teleT) {
453	        e.ai0 = 10; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 += 1;
454	        if (e.ai3 >= 9) e.ai3 = 0;
455	      }
456	      break;
457	    }
458	    case 13: { // expert 狂暴段旋转冲刺(:50108-50125;不吐泡泡)
459	      if (e.ai2 === 0) game.playSfxWav?.('Zombie_20', 1, e.cx, e.cy);   // :50112 PlaySound(29,…,20)(曾借 roar 0.6)
460	      {
461	        const r = -DUKE_SPIN_RATE * st.dir;
462	        const cos = Math.cos(r), sin = Math.sin(r);
463	        const nvx = e.vx * cos - e.vy * sin, nvy = e.vx * sin + e.vy * cos;
464	        e.vx = nvx; e.vy = nvy;
465	        e.spin -= DUKE_SPIN_RATE * st.dir;
466	      }
467	      e.ai2 += 1;
468	      if (e.ai2 >= DUKE.spinT) { e.ai0 = 10; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 += 1; }
469	      break;
470	    }
471	    default: break;
472	  }
473	  if (invuln) e.iframes = 2;
474	  e.facing = st.spr;
475	  e.x += e.vx;
476	  e.y += e.vy;
477	}
478	
479	/** 鲨鱼龙卷(状态 3 :49638-49660 / 状态 8 :49865-49885 共用):
480	 *  减速悬停,ai2==60(nadoT-30)时发射龙卷水弹 proj 385(伤害 0 的落地生成器)——
481	 *  小龙卷两发斜落(±dir*2, 8)、克苏龙卷一发原地(0,0,ai0=1=追踪型,ai2=狂暴旗)。
482	 *  落地/近身 50px 后由弹体自身 Kill 生成 384/386 龙卷,龙卷逐段向上生长并周期
483	 *  NewNPC 372/373 鲨鱼(见 DukeSharknadoBolt / DukeSharknadoTornado)。 */
484	function dukeSharknado(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player, st: DukeX, big: boolean) {
485	  e.vx *= 0.98;
486	  e.vy = lerp(e.vy, 0, 0.02);
487	  if (e.ai2 === DUKE.nadoT - 30) {
488	    if (big) {
489	      // :49871 大龙卷 = PlaySound(29,…,20) Zombie_20 满响(曾借 roar 0.7)
490	      game.playSfxWav?.('Zombie_20', 1, e.cx, e.cy);
491	      // 状态 8:克苏龙卷弹(:49873-49876;ai2=flag6 狂暴→追踪速度 +12)
492	      addProj(game, new DukeSharknadoBolt(e.cx, e.cy, 0, 0, true, dukeEnraged(game, player), e));
493	    } else {
494	      // :49644 小龙卷 = PlaySound(29,…,9) Zombie_9 满响
495	      game.playSfxWav?.('Zombie_9', 1, e.cx, e.cy);
496	      // 状态 3:左右两发斜落(:49646-49651;vector8 = rotation·(dir,0)·(w+20)/2 偏移)
497	      const ox = Math.cos(e.spin) * st.dir * (e.w + 20) / 2;
498	      addProj(game, new DukeSharknadoBolt(e.cx + ox, e.cy, st.dir * 2, 8, false, false, e));
499	      addProj(game, new DukeSharknadoBolt(e.cx + ox, e.cy, -st.dir * 2, 8, false, false, e));
500	    }
501	  }
502	  e.ai2 += 1;
503	  if (e.ai2 >= DUKE.nadoT) { e.ai0 = big ? 5 : 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; }
504	}
505	
506	/** 投射桶入桶辅助(同 bossAI.addProj;类在本文件内,避免跨模块构造注入) */
507	function addProj(game: GameHooks, p: Entity): void {
508	  (game as unknown as { entities: { add: (e: Entity, b: string) => void } }).entities.add(p, 'projectiles');
509	}
510	
511	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
512	// 龙卷水弹 385(Projectile.cs:28402-28482 AI / :70714-70778 Kill 落龙卷)
513	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
514	// aiStyle 65:ai1==0(状态 3 小弹)→ vy 余弦摆动斜落,撞块/入水/超时 Kill;
515	//  ai1>0(状态 8 克苏弹)→ 追踪玩家,速 4(+12 狂暴)+age/20,贴身 50px Kill。
516	//  Kill:小弹原地落 384 鲨鱼龙卷(伤 40);大弹自弹体 tile 向下扫描 100 格落 386
517	//  克苏龙卷(伤 80)——龙卷接触伤另按原版 hostile ×2 规则结算(Projectile.cs:13781)。
518	/** v6 联机弹体打包元数据（Boss 锚定弹体族共用形状，Game.netProjBroadcast 消费） */
519	export interface NetProjMeta {
520	  kind: number;        // msg27 kind（5-8）
521	  tag: string;         // 锚参数打包（各类自定义，≤32 字符）
522	  aux: number;         // i16 辅助通道
523	  dmg: number;         // 快照 dmg 字段
524	  /** 可选：以 vx/vy 通道携带的角度方向（重建端 atan2 还原） */
525	  exVx?: number;
526	  exVy?: number;
527	}
528	
529	export class DukeSharknadoBolt extends Entity {
530	  w = 30; h = 30; // SetDefaults 385
531	  private vx0: number;
532	  private vy0: number;
533	  private big: boolean;
534	  private enraged: boolean;
535	  /** 小弹 vy 摆动相位(原版 ai[0]) */
536	  private phase = 0;
537	  private age = 0;
538	  life = 300; // SetDefaults timeLeft
539	  /** 鲨鱼生成所需的 Duke 引用(spawnPart 位置供体,实际落点由龙卷覆写) */
540	  private src: Enemy;
541	  dead = false;
542	
543	  constructor(x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number, big: boolean, enraged: boolean, src: Enemy) {
544	    super();
545	    this.x = x - this.w / 2;
546	    this.y = y - this.h / 2;
547	    this.vx0 = vx; this.vy0 = vy;
548	    this.big = big; this.enraged = enraged;
549	    this.src = src;
550	  }
551	
552	  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks): void {
553	    if (--this.life <= 0) { this.kill(game); return; }
554	    this.age++;
555	    const p = game.player;
556	    if (this.big) {
557	      // :28405-28447 追踪:速 4(+12 狂暴)+localAI[0]/20,距玩家 <50px 即 Kill
558	      if (p && !p.dead) {
559	        const dx = p.cx - this.cx, dy = p.cy - this.cy;
560	        const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
561	        const spd = 4 + (this.enraged ? 12 : 0) + this.age / 20;
562	        this.vx0 = (dx / d) * spd;
563	        this.vy0 = (dy / d) * spd;
564	        if (d < 50) { this.kill(game); return; }
565	      }
566	    } else {
567	      // :28448-28475 vy 余弦摆动:vy += (cos(π/15·θₙ₊₁) − cos(π/15·θₙ))·4
568	      const f = (t: number) => (Math.cos((Math.PI / 15) * t) - 0.5) * 4;
569	      const d0 = f(this.phase);
570	      this.phase += 1;
571	      this.vy0 += f(this.phase) - d0;
572	    }
573	    this.x += this.vx0;
574	    this.y += this.vy0;
575	    // :28477-28481 入水上浮 16px 后 Kill
576	    const st = game.world.store;
577	    const tx = Math.floor(this.cx / TILE), ty = Math.floor(this.cy / TILE);
578	    if (st.inBounds(tx, ty) && st.liquid[st.idx(tx, ty)] > 0) { this.y -= 16; this.kill(game); return; }
579	    if (st.inBounds(tx, ty) && st.isSolid(tx, ty)) { this.kill(game); return; }
580	  }
581	
582	  /** Kill(:70714-70778):尘环 + 落龙卷(小 384 / 大 386 向下扫描地面)。
583	   *  v6 联机傀儡:运动/消亡本地自算(确定性),但不落龙卷——房主的龙卷经 kind6 同步,
584	   *  双端各自生成会重复 */
585	  private kill(game: GameHooks): void {
586	    this.dead = true;
587	    game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.cy, '#7FB8E8', 10, 2.4, { life: 24, damp: 0.94 });
588	    // :70716 弹 385 落地 = PlaySound(4,…,19) = NPC_Killed_19(case4=死音库,2026-08-19 终审补)
589	    game.playSfxWav?.('NPC_Killed_19', 1, this.cx, this.cy);
590	    if (this.netPuppet) return;
591	    if (this.big) {
592	      // :70752-70770 自弹体 tile 向下扫 100 格,落点 = 首个实心/液体格上沿 -24px
593	      const st = game.world.store;
594	      let tx = clamp(Math.floor(this.cx / TILE), 10, st.w - 10);
595	      let ty = clamp(Math.floor(this.cy / TILE), 10, st.h - 110);
596	      let ground = ty + 15;
597	      for (let y = ty; y < ty + 100; y++) {
598	        if (st.inBounds(tx, y) && (st.isSolid(tx, y) || st.liquid[st.idx(tx, y)] !== 0)) { ground = y; break; }
599	      }
600	      addProj(game, new DukeSharknadoTornado(tx * TILE + 8, ground * TILE - 24, true, this.src));
601	    } else {
602	      // :70733-70737 原地落 384(中心反向偏移 direction*30, y-4)
603	      const dir = Math.sign(this.vx0) || 1;
604	      addProj(game, new DukeSharknadoTornado(this.cx - dir * 30, this.cy - 4, false, this.src));
605	    }
606	  }
607	
608	  /** v6 联机打包：kind=5，tag=big 标记（访客重建后本地跑确定性运动，见 kill 傀儡门禁）。
609	   *  终审 M1：速度经 exVx/exVy 通道携带（本类运动用私有 vx0/vy0，Entity.vx/vy 恒 0——
610	   *  不补则访客重建的小弹水平静止原地摆到寿终） */
611	  netProjMeta(): NetProjMeta {
612	    return { kind: 5, tag: this.big ? '1' : '0', aux: 0, dmg: 0, exVx: this.vx0, exVy: this.vy0 };
613	  }
614	
615	  draw(r: Renderer, _cam: Camera): void {
616	    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');
617	    if (!ctx) return;
618	    ctx.fillStyle = '#8FD8FF';
619	    ctx.beginPath();
620	    ctx.arc(this.cx, this.cy, 6, 0, TAU);
621	    ctx.fill();
622	  }
623	}
624	
625	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
626	// 鲨鱼龙卷 384 / 克苏龙卷 386(Projectile.cs:28287-28398,aiStyle 64)
627	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
628	// 原版是"父段 ai0 倒计时到 1 → 在正上方生成子段(ai0=10, ai1-1)直至 ai1=0"的投射物塔;
629	// 本实现以单实体模拟该塔:列顶随段生长上移,鲨鱼在"新段出现且 ai1 % 出鲨模 == 0"时
630	// 于列顶 NewNPC(小 384:模 4 → 3 只/塔;大 386:模 2 → 12 只/塔,ai2=幅宽 ai3=-1.5)。
631	// 接触伤害:小 40 / 大 80(经典 NewProjectile 值),命中按原版 hostile 弹 ×2 规则(:13781)。
632	export class DukeSharknadoTornado extends Entity {
633	  private big: boolean;
634	  private ai0: number;   // 段生长倒计时(父段 16,子段 10)
635	  private ai1: number;   // 剩余段数(小 15 / 大 24)
636	  private ai1Start: number;
637	  /** 列顶中心 y(鲨鱼生成点/绘制塔顶) */
638	  private topY: number;
639	  private segs = 0;      // 已生长段数
640	  private age = 0;
641	  private alpha = 255;   // 原版 alpha(实体内嵌 SolidCollision 逐渐显形)
642	  life: number;          // timeLeft 小 540 / 大 840
643	  private src: Enemy;
644	  dead = false;
645	
646	  constructor(x: number, y: number, big: boolean, src: Enemy) {
647	    super();
648	    this.big = big;
649	    this.ai0 = 16;                       // NewProjectile ai0
650	    this.ai1 = big ? 24 : 15;            // NewProjectile ai1
651	    this.ai1Start = this.ai1;
652	    this.topY = y;
653	    this.life = big ? 840 : 540;         // SetDefaults timeLeft
654	    this.src = src;
655	    this.x = x; this.y = y;
656	    this.resize();
657	  }
658	
659	  /** 段缩放(aiStyle 64 :28297-28316):小 (25-ai1)/25;大 (32-ai1)*1.5/32 */
660	  private scaleOf(ai1: number): number {
661	    return this.big ? (32 - ai1) * 1.5 / 32 : (25 - ai1) / 25;
662	  }
663	  private resize(): void {
664	    const s = this.scaleOf(this.ai1);
665	    this.w = Math.round(150 * s);        // SetDefaults 150×42 × scale
666	    this.h = Math.round(42 * s);
667	    this.x = this.cx - this.w / 2;
668	    this.y = this.topY - this.h / 2;
669	  }
670	
671	  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks): void {
672	    if (--this.life <= 0) { this.dead = true; return; }
673	    this.age++;
674	    // alpha(:28336-28360):嵌实体块渐隐,否则向 60(大 100)显形
675	    const st = game.world.store;
676	    const inSolidTile = st.inBounds(Math.floor(this.cx / TILE), Math.floor(this.cy / TILE)) && st.isSolid(Math.floor(this.cx / TILE), Math.floor(this.cy / TILE));
677	    if (inSolidTile) this.alpha = Math.min(150, this.alpha + 30);
678	    else this.alpha = Math.max(this.big ? 100 : 60, this.alpha - 30);
679	
680	    if (this.ai0 > 0) this.ai0--;
681	    if (this.ai0 === 1 && this.ai1 > 0) {
682	      // :28363-28390 子段落点:列顶再上移 (42·sP + 42·sC)/2 − 2
683	      const sP = this.scaleOf(this.ai1), sC = this.scaleOf(this.ai1 - 1);
684	      this.topY -= (42 * sP) / 2 + (42 * sC) / 2 - 2;
685	      // 出鲨(:28372-28385):ai1 % 模 == 0 且 ai1≠0 → NewNPC 372/373 于列顶。
686	      // v6 傀儡不出鲨——房主的鲨鱼经 msg23 同步,双端各出会翻倍
687	      const mod = this.big ? 2 : 4;
688	      if (!this.netPuppet && this.ai1 % mod === 0 && this.ai1 !== 0) {
689	        const shark = spawnPart(game, this.src, this.big ? 373 : 372);
690	        if (shark) {
691	          shark.x = this.cx - shark.w / 2;
692	          shark.y = this.topY - shark.h / 2;
693	          shark.vx = this.vx; shark.vy = this.vy; // Main.npc.velocity = 弹体速度
694	          shark.bInit = false;                    // 由 sharkronAI 按 ai2/ai3 初始化
695	          shark.ai2 = this.big ? this.w : 0;      // 373 横游幅宽(:28382)
696	          shark.ai3 = this.big ? -1.5 : 0;        // 373 升速(:28383)
697	        }
698	      }
699	      this.ai1 -= 1;
700	      this.ai0 = 10;
701	      this.segs += 1;
702	      this.resize();
703	    }
704	    if (this.ai0 <= 0 && this.ai1 <= 0) {
705	      // :28392-28398 停止生长后横向摇摆(cos(π/30·−ai0) − 0.5)·幅宽
706	      const amp = (this.w / 5) * (this.big ? 2 : 1);
707	      const f = (t: number) => (Math.cos((Math.PI / 30) * t) - 0.5) * amp;
708	      this.x -= f(-this.ai0) * -1;
709	      this.ai0 -= 1;
710	      this.x += f(-this.ai0) * -1;
711	    }
712	
713	    // 接触伤害(hostile 弹 ×2 规则,Projectile.cs:13781:小 40→80 / 大 80→160)
714	    // 判定盒 = 整列(基座→列顶),宽取当前最宽段(= this.w,列顶段)
715	    const p = game.player;
716	    if (p && !p.dead) {
717	      const halfW = this.w / 2;
718	      const colTop = Math.min(this.topY, this.cy);
719	      const colH = Math.abs(this.cy - this.topY) + this.h;
720	      if (Math.abs(p.cx - this.cx) < halfW + p.w / 2 && Math.abs(p.cy - (colTop + colH / 2)) < colH / 2 + p.h / 2) {
721	        // 弹幕命中：attacker 仅做死亡归因，contactOnly 缺省 false → 荆棘不反伤
722	        // （反伤只在 NPC 接触分支 Player.cs:30910-30944）
723	        game.damagePlayer(this.big ? 160 : 80, this.cx, this.cy, this.src);
724	      }
725	    }
726	  }
727	
728	  /** v6 联机打包：kind=6，tag=`${big},${剩余段数ai1},${段倒计时ai0}`。
729	   *  近似收紧②：携带生长状态——中途入视野的龙卷按快照段盒+段数确定性恢复
730	   *  （topY = 快照y + 当前段半高,与房主同公式）,不再从零重长 */
731	  netProjMeta() {
732	    return { kind: 6, tag: `${this.big ? 1 : 0},${this.ai1},${this.ai0}`, aux: 0, dmg: 0 };
733	  }
734	
735	  /** v6 近似收紧②：访客重建时由快照段盒 + ai1/ai0 恢复生长态。
736	   *  topY 与 ai1 是确定性函数关系（resize: y = topY − h(ai1)/2），故由
737	   *  snapY 反解即可精确对齐房主当前态（含列顶位置,判定盒不再错位） */
738	  netStateSync(ai1: number, ai0: number, snapX: number, snapY: number): void {
739	    if (!Number.isFinite(ai1) || ai1 < 0 || ai1 > this.ai1Start) return; // 非法态保持新建
740	    this.ai1 = ai1;
741	    this.ai0 = Number.isFinite(ai0) ? ai0 : 10;
742	    this.segs = this.ai1Start - ai1;
743	    this.resize();
744	    this.x = snapX;                    // cx = snapX + w/2,resize 后 x = cx − w/2 = snapX
745	    this.topY = snapY + this.h / 2;    // 反解列顶（y = topY − h/2 的逆）
746	    this.y = snapY;
747	  }
748	
749	  draw(r: Renderer, _cam: Camera): void {
750	    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');
751	    if (!ctx) return;
752	    ctx.save();
753	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1 - this.alpha / 255;
754	    // 塔形:自基座向列顶逐段变宽(384 族贴图未解包,按段缩放画半透明白蓝柱)
755	    let segY = this.cy + this.h / 2;
756	    for (let i = this.segs; i >= 0; i--) {
757	      const s = this.scaleOf(Math.max(0, this.ai1Start - i));
758	      const sw = 150 * s, sh = 42 * s;
759	      segY -= sh;
760	      ctx.fillStyle = i % 2 ? '#9FD4F0' : '#7FB8E8';
761	      ctx.fillRect(this.cx - sw / 2, segY, sw, sh);
762	    }
763	    ctx.restore();
764	  }
765	}
766	
767	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
768	// 鲨鱼 NPC aiStyle 71(NPC 372 Sharkron / 373,NPC.cs:35196-35328)
769	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
770	// ai0=0:ai3 升速垂直蓄力 90t(dontTakeDamage;373 另带 ai2 幅宽余弦横游+渐显);
771	// 期满 ai0=1 → 16 速直扑玩家;ai0=1:渐显(−60/t)、撞实心块即死、60t 后恢复重力。
772	export function sharkronAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null): void {
773	  if (!e.bInit) {
774	    e.bInit = true;
775	    e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0;
776	    e.spawnAlpha = 255; e.alphaFade = false; // 渐显由本 AI 按 :35228/:35267 驱动
777	  }
778	  // :35200-35205 玩家失效 → TargetClosest(faceTarget:false)+direction=1 重瞄,
779	  // AI 照常运转(原版对尸位坐标继续扑,不消散——曾 dead=true 即散 = 存续断裂,
780	  // 2026-08-19 台账 E 修;本仓单玩家无处重瞄 → 置 direction=1、维持现速滑行)
781	  if (!player || player.dead) {
782	    e.facing = 1;
783	    player = null;
784	  }
785	  const RISE_T = 90; // num1077
786	  if (e.ai0 === 0) {
787	    e.iframes = 2;              // dontTakeDamage(:35211)
788	    e.vx = 0;
789	    e.vy = e.ai3;               // velocity.Y = ai[3](:35212)
790	    if (e.vanillaId === 373) {
791	      e.spawnAlpha = Math.max(0, e.spawnAlpha - 6); // :35228 渐显
792	      // :35222-35227 贴图倾斜 rotation = vy·spriteDirection·0.1(钳 ±0.2)
793	      e.spin = clamp(e.vy * -e.facing * 0.1, -0.2, 0.2);
794	    }
795	    e.ai1 += 1;
796	    if (e.ai1 >= RISE_T) {
797	      // :35242-35261 蓄力期满:16 速扑咬(撞块内则 ai1=0 延后起扑);
798	      // :35250 PlaySound(4,…,19)=**NPCKilled** style19(case4=死音库!曾误用
799	      // NPC_Hit_19 错库,2026-08-19 终审修)
800	      e.ai0 = 1;
801	      e.ai1 = inSolid(game, e) ? 0 : 1;
802	      game.playSfxFiles?.(['NPC_Killed_19'], 1, e.cx, e.cy);
803	      if (player) {
804	        const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
805	        const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
806	        e.vx = (dx / d) * 16;
807	        e.vy = (dy / d) * 16;
808	        e.facing = dx < 0 ? -1 : 1;
809	        e.spin = Math.atan2(e.vy, e.vx) + (e.facing < 0 ? Math.PI : 0);
810	      }
811	    }
812	  } else {
813	    // ai0 === 1(:35264-35316)
814	    if (e.ai1 < 1 && !inSolid(game, e)) e.ai1 = 1;
815	    if (inSolid(game, e)) e.spawnAlpha = Math.min(150, e.spawnAlpha + 15); // :35280-35284
816	    if (e.ai1 >= 1) {
817	      e.spawnAlpha = Math.max(0, e.spawnAlpha - 60); // :35288 渐显
818	      e.ai1 += 1;
819	      if (inSolid(game, e)) {
820	        // :35295-35303 撞墙即死:PlaySound(DeathSound)——372/373 SetDefaults
821	        // (:13666-13688)DeathSound=NPCDeath1 → NPC_Killed_1
822	        game.playSfxFiles(['NPC_Killed_1'], 1, e.cx, e.cy);
823	        e.dead = true;
824	        return;
825	      }
826	    }
827	    if (e.ai1 >= 60) e.applyGravity(game); // :35307-35309 恢复重力(共享 UpdateNPC_UpdateGravity)
828	    e.spin = Math.atan2(e.vy, e.vx) + (e.facing < 0 ? Math.PI : 0); // :35311-35315
829	  }
830	  // 373 蓄力段横游(:35213-35221):x += (cos(π/30·l1) − 0.5)·ai2·(−direction) 位移差
831	  if (e.ai0 === 0 && e.vanillaId === 373 && e.ai2 > 0) {
832	    const f = (t: number) => (Math.cos((Math.PI / 30) * t) - 0.5) * e.ai2;
833	    const prev = f(e.ai1 - 1), cur = f(e.ai1);
834	    e.x += (cur - prev) * -e.facing;
835	  }
836	  e.x += e.vx;
837	  e.y += e.vy;
838	}
839	
840	/** AI_070 爆裂泡泡(NPC 371,NPC.cs:35122-35195):
841	 *  朝玩家漂移(风偏+微上浮),玩家贴近(外扩 40px)或 150t 后爆炸(体积扩到 100×100,
842	 *  4t 后消散)。原版命中是 justHit→爆炸而非死亡;本工程近似为 hp 受损即转爆炸。
843	 *  简化:ai3 的随机 scale(:35134)无 Enemy 对应字段,仅记录不应用。 */
844	export function dukeBubbleAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
845	  void game;
846	  // :35180-35194 受击（justHit 单帧旗标）→ 无敌 + 爆炸体积；hp 损失本身不触发——
847	  // 真身是 StrikeNPC cs:82102-82107（371 伤害清零、ai0=1/ai1=4/dontTakeDamage），
848	  // 已由 Enemy.hurt() 同构处理，此处 justHit 为 AI 侧兜底
849	  if (e.ai0 !== 1 && e.justHit) { e.ai0 = 1; e.ai1 = 4; }
850	  // ai0/ai1 复位先于任何读点(原在 player 门内——无玩家时 ai0 留 -1120 会让
851	  // :844 `ai0===0` 落空直入爆炸分支 1 tick 即死;2026-08-13 ai0 清查)
852	  if (!e.bInit) { e.bInit = true; e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; }
853	  if (e.bInit && player && e.ai3 <= 0) { // :35124-35131 初速 = 朝玩家 ±100px 随机偏移 × 11~17.7 速
854	    e.ai3 = (80 + Math.random() * 41) / 100;
855	    const jx = e.cx - player.cx + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 200;
856	    const jy = e.cy - player.cy + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 200;
857	    const d = Math.hypot(jx, jy) || 1;
858	    const spd = (165 + Math.random() * 100) / 15;
859	    e.vx = -(jx / d) * spd;
860	    e.vy = -(jy / d) * spd;
861	    e.alphaFade = false;
862	    e.spawnAlpha = 50; // :35136-35140
863	  }
864	  if (player) {
865	    // :35132-35133 主漂移:v = (v*40 + dir*20)/41
866	    const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
867	    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
868	    e.vx = (e.vx * 40 + (dx / d) * 20) / 41;
869	    e.vy = (e.vy * 40 + (dy / d) * 20) / 41;
870	  }
871	  // :35141-35146 风扰+上浮(风取 0)
872	  e.vx = (e.vx * 50 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2) / 51;
873	  e.vy = (e.vy * 50 - 0.25 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 4) / 51;
874	  if (e.vy > 0) e.vy -= 0.04;
875	  if (e.ai0 === 0 && player) { // :35147-35175 玩家贴近 → 爆炸
876	    const pad = 40 + e.w / 2;
877	    if (Math.abs(player.cx - e.cx) < pad + player.w / 2 && Math.abs(player.cy - e.cy) < pad + player.h / 2) {
878	      e.ai0 = 1; e.ai1 = 4;
879	    }
880	  }
881	  if (e.ai0 === 0) {
882	    e.ai1 += 1;
883	    if (e.ai1 >= 150) { e.ai0 = 1; e.ai1 = 4; } // :35167-35175 超时自爆
884	  } else {
885	    // 爆炸:体积扩到 100×100(:35187-35193)并 4t 后消散
886	    if (e.w !== 100) {
887	      e.x = e.cx - 50; e.y = e.cy - 50;
888	      e.w = 100; e.h = 100;
889	      game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, '#BFE8FF', 8, 2, { life: 20 });
890	    }
891	    e.iframes = 2; // dontTakeDamage
892	    e.ai1 -= 1;
893	    if (e.ai1 <= 0) { e.dead = true; return; }
894	  }
895	  e.x += e.vx;
896	  e.y += e.vy;
897	}

899	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
900	// 月亮领主 aiStyle 77 核心(NPC 398,NPC.cs:36774-37198)
901	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
902	// ai0:-1 出生演出(60t,生成 2×397 手 + 1×396 头)→ 0 一阶段(双手+头存活期间无敌,随玩家悬停)
903	//   → 1 二阶段(三部件全部闭眼(ai0=-2)后解除无敌,自主追玩家+弹幕循环)
904	//   → 2 死亡演出(600t 后真死,全族消散)/ 3 玩家全灭离场(60t 后全族清除)。
905	//   -2:远距(>2400px)传送归位后的短演出。
906	
907	/** 二阶段弹幕表 = MoonLordAttacksArray2(:6966-6985,10 槽循环),驱动 NPC 400 真眼
908	 *  (aiStyle 81,:38243-38264,由部件 checkDead 召出,见 spawnTrueEye)。
909	 *  真眼数据/贴图缺失导致 spawn 失败时,攻击主链才折叠到核心上执行兜底。 */
910	const ML_CORE_P2: ReadonlyArray<readonly [number, number]> = [
911	  [0, 90], [1, 90], [0, 90], [2, 135], [0, 90], [3, 200], [0, 90], [4, 375], [0, 90], [2, 135],
912	];
913	
914	/** 朝玩家发射一发弹幕的通用出口(伤害/弹 id 全部来自原版 NewProjectile 调用点) */
915	function mlShoot(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player, ox: number, oy: number,
916	  spd: number, dmg: number, projId: number) {
917	  const dx = player.cx - (e.cx + ox), dy = player.cy - (e.cy + oy);
918	  const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
919	  shoot(game, e.cx + ox, e.cy + oy, (dx / d) * spd, (dy / d) * spd, dmg, projId, 600);
920	}
921	
922	/** checkDead(:78845-78857):手 397 / 头 396 血尽 → ai0=-2 闭眼残肢的同时,于部件中心
923	 *  召 NPC 400 真眼,ai[3] = 部件的 ai[3](核心 id,游离眼 AI 据此锚定核心) */
924	function spawnTrueEye(game: GameHooks, part: Enemy): void {
925	  const eye = spawnPart(game, part, 400);
926	  if (!eye) return;
927	  eye.x = part.cx - eye.w / 2;
928	  eye.y = part.cy - eye.h / 2;
929	  eye.ai0 = 0; eye.ai1 = 0; eye.ai2 = 0; eye.ai3 = part.ai3;
930	}
931	
932	/** 月噬弹 456（Projectile.cs:5008 SetDefaults + aiStyle 85 :30678-30750）：
933	 *  16×16 / hostile / 0 伤 / noTileCollide / timeLeft 600。ai[0]>0 出击段：每 tick 朝
934	 *  玩家 min(16, 距离) 直飞，贴身 <20px 时 AddBuff(145 MoonLeech, 840t/专家 960t)
935	 *  （:30722-30732，本仓经典模式恒 840t = 14s）；age≥330 或玩家失联 → ai[0] 翻负
936	 *  回程段：朝 额前(0,+216) 直飞，<20px Kill（吸血视觉闭环）。
937	 *  弹体自旋角 = (源心 - 玩家心 + (0,216)).ToRotation() + π/2（:30717）。 */
938	export class MoonLeechProj extends Entity {
939	  w = 16; h = 16;
940	  vx = 0; vy = 0;
941	  private srcId: number;
942	  private out = true;      // ai[0] 符号：true = 出击 / false = 回程
943	  private age = 0;         // localAI[0]
944	  private fed = false;     // localAI[1]（已吸血，不重复授 buff）
945	  private life = 600;      // timeLeft
946	  dead = false;
947	
948	  constructor(src: Enemy, player: Player) {
949	    super();
950	    this.srcId = src.id;
951	    this.x = src.cx - this.w / 2;
952	    this.y = src.cy + 216 - this.h / 2;   // 额前 (0,+216)
953	    const dx = player.cx - this.cx, dy = player.cy - this.cy;
954	    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
955	    this.vx = (dx / d) * 6; this.vy = (dy / d) * 6;   // :37823 出速 6（AI 段接管为 16 上限）
956	  }
957	
958	  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks): void {
959	    const src = (game.enemies() as Enemy[]).find((n) => n.id === this.srcId);
960	    const player = game.player;
961	    if (--this.life <= 0 || !src || src.dead) { this.dead = true; return; }
962	    if (!player || player.dead) { if (this.out) this.out = false; }   // :30706-30712 玩家失联即回程
963	    this.age++;
964	    if (this.age >= 330 && this.out) this.out = false;                // :30702-30705
965	    // 出击：朝玩家心直飞（:30722-30725）；回程：朝源额前 (0,+216)（:30734-30738）
966	    const tx = this.out ? player.cx : src.cx;
967	    const ty = this.out ? player.cy : src.cy + 216;
968	    const dx = tx - this.cx, dy = ty - this.cy;
969	    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
970	    const spd = Math.min(16, d);
971	    if (d > 0.001) { this.vx = (dx / d) * spd; this.vy = (dy / d) * spd; }
972	    this.x += this.vx; this.y += this.vy;
973	    // 贴身结算：出击段 <20px 授 MoonLeech 145（:30726-30732）；回程段 <20px Kill（:30740-30743）
974	    if (this.out && d < 20 && !this.fed) {
975	      this.fed = true;
976	      player.buffs.apply(BuffType.MoonLeech, 840 / 60);
977	    } else if (!this.out && d < 20) {
978	      this.dead = true;
979	    }
980	  }
981	
982	  draw(r: Renderer, _cam: Camera): void {
983	    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');
984	    if (!ctx) return;
985	    const img = projSprite(456);
986	    if (!img || !(img.width > 0) || img.width === 0) return;
987	    // 自旋（:30717）：(源心 - 玩家心 + (0,216)) 角 + π/2——回程矢量已同向，直接用速度角近似
988	    const ang = Math.atan2(this.vy, this.vx) + Math.PI / 2;
989	    ctx.save();
990	    ctx.translate(this.cx, this.cy);
991	    ctx.rotate(ang);
992	    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
993	    // 多帧行切片（Main.projFrames[456]=4，Main.cs:8490；aiStyle 85 不推帧 → 恒帧 0，
994	    // 帧格 22×24）。曾整条 22×96 压进 16×16 判定盒 = 胶片条（G11）
995	    const frames = projFrameCount(456);
996	    const fh = img.height / frames;
997	    ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, img.width, fh, -img.width / 2, -fh / 2,
998	      img.width, fh);
999	    ctx.restore();
1000	  }
1001	}
1002	
1003	/** 月噬怪召出(:37828-37842 残肢 / :37975-37989 月噬攻击):头在 lt/inSlot=120/180/240
1004	 *  于玩家处召 NPC 401,ai[0]=头 whoAmI+1 / ai[1]=源弹 456 索引。
1005	 *  原版门禁 = 遍历全部 456 弹,其 ai[1] 指向的玩家 FindBuffIndex(145) != -1 才召
1006	 *  （每弹一只）。本仓单玩家:debuff 145 已由 MoonLeechProj 贴身授予 → 玩家身中
1007	 *  MoonLeech 即召,等价替换旧「弹已射出即命中」无条件近似
1008	 *  (ai[1] 源弹索引以 -1 代,moonLeechAI 以出生点代弹着点,不受影响)。 */
1009	function spawnMoonLeech(game: GameHooks, head: Enemy, player: Player): void {
1010	  if (!player.buffs.moonLeech) return;
1011	  const leech = spawnPart(game, head, 401);
1012	  if (!leech) return;
1013	  leech.x = player.cx - leech.w / 2;
1014	  leech.y = player.cy - leech.h / 2;
1015	  leech.ai0 = head.id + 1;
1016	  leech.ai1 = -1;
1017	  leech.ai2 = 0;
1018	  leech.vx = 0; leech.vy = 0;
1019	}
1020	
1021	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
1022	// 死亡之光 455 PhantasmalDeathray(Projectile.cs:30291-30549,aiStyle 84)
1023	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
1024	// 定轴扫射激光:锚定源 NPC 眼部(头 396 椭圆 (27,59)·睁眼度 / 真眼 400 椭圆 (30,30)),
1025	// 每 tick 转 ai0(=±2π/540),寿命 180t,scale = sin(age·π/180)·10·maxScale 脉冲张合;
1026	// 束长 LaserScan(3 采样,最大 2400)以 0.5/t Lerp 逼近;命中判定 = 玩家矩形 vs
1027	// 「源心 → 源心+dir·束长」线段,线宽 36·scale(Colliding :14673-14682);
1028	// 伤害 = NewProjectile 值 ×2(hostile 弹规则 :13781:头 75→150 / 真眼 50→100)。
1029	export class MLDeathray extends Entity {
1030	  w = 36; h = 36; // SetDefaults 455
1031	  private srcId: number;
1032	  /** v6 联机：锚定 NPC 的 netId（访客端锚=msg23 傀儡；0=本端真身走 srcId 查找） */
1033	  srcNetId = 0;
1034	  /** 'head' = 396 头眼 / 'core' = 400 真眼(本仓折叠到核心 398) */
1035	  private kind: 'head' | 'core';
1036	  /** 扫射角速度(原版 ai[0] = ±2π/540) */
1037	  private sweep: number;
1038	  /** 当前束角(原版 velocity.ToRotation,每 tick +sweep) */
1039	  private angle: number;
1040	  private age = 0;
1041	  /** 束长 Lerp 值(原版 localAI[1]) */
1042	  private len = 0;
1043	  private dmg: number;
1044	  dead = false;
1045	
1046	  constructor(src: Enemy | null, kind: 'head' | 'core', angle: number, sweep: number, dmg: number, x?: number, y?: number) {
1047	    super();
1048	    this.srcId = src?.id ?? 0;
1049	    this.kind = kind;
1050	    this.angle = angle;
1051	    this.sweep = sweep;
1052	    this.dmg = dmg;
1053	    // v6 傀儡重建：src=null 时由调用方传显式坐标（真身路径 src 必非空）
1054	    this.x = (x ?? src!.cx) - this.w / 2;
1055	    this.y = (y ?? src!.cy) - this.h / 2;
1056	  }
1057	
1058	  /** v6 联机打包：kind=7，tag=`${srcId},${0|1 数值位（0=head 1=core）}`，aux=扫速×10000，
1059	   *  束角经 vx/vy 通道携带（重建端 atan2 还原后本地扫射——确定性自包含） */
1060	  netProjMeta(): NetProjMeta {
1061	    return {
1062	      kind: 7,
1063	      tag: `${this.srcId},${this.kind === 'head' ? 0 : 1}`,
1064	      aux: Math.max(-32768, Math.min(32767, Math.round(this.sweep * 10000))),
1065	      dmg: this.dmg,
1066	      exVx: Math.cos(this.angle) * 1000,
1067	      exVy: Math.sin(this.angle) * 1000,
1068	    };
1069	  }
1070	
1071	  /** scale 上限:头 1.0 / 真眼 0.4(:30468-30478 num728) */
1072	  private get maxScale(): number { return this.kind === 'head' ? 1 : 0.4; }
1073	
1074	  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks): void {
1075	    // 死光出生音：aiStyle84 首帧 PlaySound(29,…,104)=Zombie_104(pcs:30405-30408,
1076	    // ×0.55 档自动;曾整段无声,2026-08-19 终审补)
1077	    if (this.age === 0) game.playSfxWav?.('Zombie_104', 1, this.cx, this.cy);
1078	    // v6 近似收紧①：束角回写——快照把房主实时角度放在 vx/vy 通道（exVx/exVy），
1079	    // 傀儡消费式同步（读后清零防下一 tick 重复回拉振荡）；快照间隙内本地按扫速
1080	    // 自推插值。后台页暂停恢复后由下一快照纠偏（此前只按首帧角自推会永久漂移）
1081	    if (this.netPuppet && (this.vx !== 0 || this.vy !== 0)) {
1082	      this.angle = Math.atan2(this.vy, this.vx);
1083	      this.vx = 0; this.vy = 0;
1084	    }
1085	    // v6 联机：傀儡端按 netId 找锚（msg23 傀儡）；本端真身按本地 id
1086	    const src = this.srcNetId
1087	      ? (game.enemies() as Enemy[]).find((n) => n.netId === this.srcNetId)
1088	      : (game.enemies() as Enemy[]).find((n) => n.id === this.srcId);
1089	    if (!src || src.dead) { this.dead = true; return; }        // 源消亡即 Kill(:30297)
1090	    if (!this.netPuppet && this.kind === 'head' && src.ai0 === -2) { this.dead = true; return; } // :30296 头闭眼残肢（ai0 不同步,傀儡跳过——msg28 兜底）
1091	    this.age++;
1092	    if (this.age >= 180) { this.dead = true; return; }         // :30472 寿命 180t
1093	    this.angle += this.sweep;                                  // :30521 rotation += ai[0]
1094	
1095	    // 锚点:束原点 = 源心 + 眼球椭圆偏移(:30293-30308)
1096	    const hx = headOf(src);
1097	    const open = this.kind === 'head' ? hx.l1 : 1;
1098	    const eyeA = this.kind === 'head' ? hx.l0 : this.angle;    // 真眼无独立瞳角,随束角
1099	    const ex = this.kind === 'head' ? Math.cos(eyeA) * 27 * open : Math.cos(eyeA) * 30;
1100	    const ey = this.kind === 'head' ? Math.sin(eyeA) * 59 * open : Math.sin(eyeA) * 30;
1101	    this.x = src.cx + ex - this.w / 2;
1102	    this.y = src.cy + ey - this.h / 2;
1103	
1104	    // 束长:LaserScan 3 采样(线宽 36·scale,最大 2400)均值,:30483-30509
1105	    const scale = Math.min(this.maxScale, Math.sin((this.age * Math.PI) / 180) * 10 * this.maxScale);
1106	    const wSq = 36 * scale;
1107	    const dirX = Math.cos(this.angle), dirY = Math.sin(this.angle);
1108	    let amt = 0.5;
1109	    let dist = this.laserScan(game, dirX, dirY, wSq);
1110	    const p = game.player;
1111	    if (this.kind === 'head' && p && !p.dead && !losClear(game, this.cx, this.cy, p.cx, p.cy)) {
1112	      // :30511-30519 头与玩家无视线 → 束长取 距玩家+150(防隔墙盲射)
1113	      dist = Math.min(2400, Math.hypot(p.cx - src.cx, p.cy - src.cy) + 150);
1114	      amt = 0.75;
1115	    }
1116	    this.len = lerp(this.len, dist, amt);
1117	
1118	    // 命中:玩家矩形 vs 束线段(线宽 36·scale),伤害走 damagePlayer(iframes 节流)。
1119	    // 弹幕命中：attacker 仅做死亡归因，contactOnly 缺省 false → 荆棘不反伤
1120	    // （反伤只在 NPC 接触分支 Player.cs:30910-30944）
1121	    if (p && !p.dead && segHitsRect(this.cx, this.cy, dirX * this.len, dirY * this.len, wSq, p)) {
1122	      game.damagePlayer(this.dmg, this.cx, this.cy, src);

1126	  /** Collision.LaserScan 近似:垂直束向 ±半宽 3 条采样线,8px 步进至首实心块 */
1127	  private laserScan(game: GameHooks, dirX: number, dirY: number, width: number): number {
1128	    const px = -dirY, py = dirX; // 垂直束向
1129	    let sum = 0;
1130	    for (let k = -1; k <= 1; k++) {
1131	      const off = (k * width) / 2;
1132	      const ox = this.cx + px * off, oy = this.cy + py * off;
1133	      const steps = Math.ceil(2400 / 8);
1134	      let d = 2400;
1135	      for (let i = 1; i <= steps; i++) {
1136	        const t = i * 8;
1137	        const tx = Math.floor((ox + dirX * t) / TILE), ty = Math.floor((oy + dirY * t) / TILE);
1138	        const st = game.world.store;
1139	        if (!st.inBounds(tx, ty)) { d = t - 8; break; }
1140	        if (st.isSolid(tx, ty)) { d = t - 8; break; }
1141	      }
1142	      sum += d;
1143	    }
1144	    return sum / 3;
1145	  }
1146	
1147	  draw(r: Renderer, _cam: Camera): void {
1148	    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');
1149	    if (!ctx) return;
1150	    const scale = Math.min(this.maxScale, Math.sin((this.age * Math.PI) / 180) * 10 * this.maxScale);
1151	    if (scale <= 0.01 || this.len <= 1) return;
1152	    const thick = Math.max(2, 36 * scale);
1153	    ctx.save();
1154	    ctx.globalAlpha = clamp(scale * 1.6, 0.25, 1);
1155	    ctx.translate(this.cx, this.cy);
1156	    ctx.rotate(this.angle);
1157	    ctx.fillStyle = '#B040D0';
1158	    ctx.fillRect(0, -thick / 2, this.len, thick);
1159	    ctx.globalAlpha = clamp(scale * 0.9, 0.15, 0.6);
1160	    ctx.fillStyle = '#F0C8FF';
1161	    ctx.fillRect(0, -thick / 4, this.len, thick / 2);
1162	    ctx.restore();
1163	  }
1164	}

1166	/** 线段 vs AABB(原版 Collision.CheckAABBvLineCollision 语义:线宽胶囊与矩形相交) */
1167	function segHitsRect(x0: number, y0: number, dx: number, dy: number, width: number,
1168	  t: { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number }): boolean {
1169	  // 最近点参数 t∈[0,1](钳到段内)
1170	  const cx = t.x + t.w / 2, cy = t.y + t.h / 2;
1171	  const len2 = dx * dx + dy * dy || 1;
1172	  let k = ((cx - x0) * dx + (cy - y0) * dy) / len2;
1173	  k = clamp(k, 0, 1);
1174	  const px = x0 + dx * k, py = y0 + dy * k;
1175	  // 最近点到矩形距离(逐轴钳位)
1176	  const nx = clamp(px, t.x, t.x + t.w), ny = clamp(py, t.y, t.y + t.h);
1177	  return Math.hypot(px - nx, py - ny) < width / 2;
1178	}
1179	
1180	/** 死亡之光发射出口(原版 :37902-37916 头 / :38625-38637 真眼):
1181	 *  起角 = 朝玩家角 ∓ 60°(num1278 = 玩家在左 +1),角速度 = num1278·2π/540。
1182	 *  返回源 NPC 的扫射编码角初值((起角+3π)·num1278,头部 ai2 逐 t 解码步进用)。 */
1183	function mlDeathray(game: GameHooks, src: Enemy, kind: 'head' | 'core', player: Player, dmg: number): number {
1184	  const dx = player.cx - src.cx, dy = player.cy - src.cy;
1185	  const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
1186	  const n = dx / d < 0 ? 1 : -1;                       // num1278
1187	  const ang = Math.atan2(dy, dx) - n * (TAU / 6);
1188	  addProj(game, new MLDeathray(src, kind, ang, n * (TAU / 540), dmg));
1189	  return (ang + Math.PI * 3) * n;
1190	}

1192	/** 核心悬停移动(:36854-36865 / :36912-36923 同构):目标 = 玩家 + (0,130),8 速/0.5 步进+半程混合 */
1193	function mlCoreHover(e: Enemy, player: Player) {
1194	  const dx = player.cx - e.cx;
1195	  const dy = player.cy + 130 - e.cy;
1196	  if (Math.hypot(dx, dy) > 20) {
1197	    const d = Math.hypot(dx - e.vx, dy - e.vy) || 1;
1198	    flyLerp(e, ((dx - e.vx) / d) * 8, ((dy - e.vy) / d) * 8, 0.5);
1199	  }
1200	}

1202	/** :36934-36952(死亡演出 ai1==60)/:37074-37099(玩家全灭离场 ai1==40)清弹段:
1203	 *  全部 456/462/455/452/454 弹 + NPC 400(原版前者 Kill()/后者 active=false——
1204	 *  本仓统一 dead=true 出桶) */
1205	function mlClearDeathrayProjectiles(game: GameHooks): void {
1206	  const ents = (game as unknown as {
1207	    entities?: { projectiles?: Array<{ dead?: boolean; projId?: number }> };
1208	  }).entities;
1209	  for (const p of ents?.projectiles ?? []) {
1210	    if (p.dead) continue;
1211	    const id = p.projId;
1212	    if (id === 456 || id === 462 || id === 455 || id === 452 || id === 454) p.dead = true;
1213	  }
1214	  for (const n of game.enemies() as Enemy[]) {
1215	    if (!n.dead && n.vanillaId === 400) n.dead = true;
1216	  }
1217	}

1219	export function moonLordCoreAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
1220	  // :36776-36778 1/200 环境吼(PlaySound 29 style Next(93,100) = Zombie_93..99,

1224	  }
1225	  if (!e.bInit) { e.bInit = true; e.ai0 = -1; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; } // :36780-36785
1226	  // 原版 checkDead(:78859-78867):血尽 → ai0=2 死亡演出,满血续命 600t 后真死
1227	  if (e.hp <= 0 && e.ai0 !== 2) { e.ai0 = 2; e.ai1 = 0; e.hp = e.maxHp; e.iframes = 2; }

1231	    e.ai1 += 1;
1232	    if (e.ai1 >= 60) { e.ai1 = 0; e.ai0 = 0; }
1233	  } else if (e.ai0 === -1) { // 出生演出(:36810-36853)
1234	    e.iframes = 2;
1235	    e.ai1 += 1;
1236	    if (e.ai1 >= 60) {
1237	      e.ai1 = 0; e.ai0 = 0; e.ai2 = 0;
1238	      // 生成双手 397(核心两侧 ±400)与头 396(上方 400);ai3 = 核心 id(锚定)
1239	      for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) {

1244	          h.ai0 = 0; h.ai1 = 0; h.ai2 = i; h.ai3 = e.id; h.bInit = true;

1246	      }

1254	    }

1293	    if (e.ai1 === 1) game.playSfxFiles?.(['NPC_Killed_61'], 1, e.cx, e.cy);

1299	    if (e.ai1 >= 600) { // :37053-37063 真死 → life=0+checkDead 走完整 NPCLoot
1300	      // ★须 die() 而非直写 dead——hurt 对 398 放行(血尽转演出),直写=标准

1390	          shoot(game, e.cx + ux * 17, e.cy + uy * 17, ux * 8, uy * 8, 35, 452, 600);

1393	      break;

1395	    case 4: // 死亡之光 455(:38625-38647):inSlot==180 一发定轴扫射激光(50 伤→命中×2=100)
1396	      // 原版此槽 375t:0-179 蓄能尘,180 发束,束体自转 180t 至槽末;真眼瞳角动画随束同步
1397	      // (localAI[0],:38642-38652)——真眼不在本仓范围,锚点用核心中心 (30,30) 椭圆近似
1398	      if (inSlot === 180) { e.ai2 = mlDeathray(game, e, 'core', player, 50 * 2); }

1405	// 月亮领主 aiStyle 78 手(NPC 397,NPC.cs:37199-37711)

1414	const ML_HAND_ATTACKS: Readonly<Record<number, ReadonlyArray<readonly [number, number]>>> = {
1415	  0: [[0, 50], [1, 70], [2, 330], [0, 60], [3, 90]],
1416	  1: [[1, 70], [0, 50], [3, 90], [0, 60], [2, 330]],

1419	export function moonLordHandAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
1420	  // 锚定核心;核心缺失/死亡 → 自毁(:37202-37207)
1421	  const core = (game.enemies() as Enemy[]).find((n) => n.id === e.ai3 && n.vanillaId === 398);
1422	  if (!core || core.dead) { e.dead = true; return; }
1423	  // 原版 checkDead(:78845-78857):血尽 → ai0=-2 闭眼残肢(满血续命+无敌)+召真眼 400
1424	  if (e.hp <= 0 && e.ai0 !== -2) {
1425	    e.ai0 = -2; e.ai1 = 0; e.hp = e.maxHp; e.iframes = 2;
1426	    spawnTrueEye(game, e);
1427	  }
1428	
1429	  const x = handOf(e);
1430	  const side = e.ai2 === 0 ? -1 : 1;  // num1231 :37208-37210
1431	  e.facing = side;                    // spriteDirection
1432	
1433	  // 攻击表推进(:37221-37251;ai0=-2 残肢态不参与调度)
1434	  let inSlot = 0, dur = 0;
1435	  if (e.ai0 !== -2) {
1436	    e.ai1 += 1;
1437	    const s = attackSlot(e, ML_HAND_ATTACKS[e.ai2 === 0 ? 0 : 1]);
1438	    e.ai0 = s.atk; // :37245 原版 ai[0] 即攻击号(亦为手部状态)
1439	    inSlot = s.inSlot; dur = s.dur;
1440	  }
1441	  let frameT = 0; // num1234(帧目标=frameT*7)
1442	
1443	  if (e.ai0 === -2) { // 闭眼残肢(:37252-37280)
1444	    frameT = 0;
1445	    e.def.damage = 80; // :37254
1446	    if (e.ai1 >= 32) e.ai1 = 0;
1447	    handHover(e, core, side);
1448	  } else if (e.ai0 === 0) { // 闭眼悬停(:37281-37303)
1449	    frameT = 3;
1450	    x.l1 = Math.max(0, x.l1 - 0.05);
1451	    handHover(e, core, side);
1452	  } else if (e.ai0 === 1 && player) { // 眼球环射(:37304-37346)
1453	    frameT = 0;
1454	    const cyc = 28; // num1240*num1241 = 7*4
1455	    if (inSlot >= cyc * 2) {
1456	      x.l1 = Math.max(0, x.l1 - 0.07);
1457	    } else if (inSlot >= cyc) {
1458	      x.l1 = Math.min(0.75, x.l1 + 0.05);
1459	      const ang = (TAU * (inSlot % cyc)) / cyc - Math.PI / 2; // :37324
1460	      x.l0 = Math.atan2(Math.sin(ang) * 66, Math.cos(ang) * 30);
1461	      if (inSlot % 4 === 0) handFireEye(e, game, x, side); // :37326-37334 452(30 伤)
1462	    } else {
1463	      x.l1 = Math.min(0.75, x.l1 + 0.02);
1464	      const ang = (TAU * (inSlot % cyc)) / cyc - Math.PI / 2;
1465	      x.l0 = Math.atan2(Math.sin(ang) * 66, Math.cos(ang) * 30);
1466	    }
1467	  } else if (e.ai0 === 2) { // 能量球扫射(:37347-37447)
1468	    const ax = core.cx + 220 * side + side * 100;   // vector168
1469	    const ay = core.cy - 60 - 50;
1470	    const swx = 400 * side, swy = -60;              // vector169
1471	    if (inSlot < 30) {
1472	      handApproach(e, ax, ay, 8, 0.2);
1473	    } else if (inSlot < 210) {
1474	      frameT = 1;
1475	      if ((inSlot - 30) % 30 === 0 && player) { // :37372-37380 每 30t 一发 454(40 伤)
1476	        const k = Math.floor((inSlot - 30) / 30);
1477	        let vx = 5 * side + (k - 3.5) * side * 3;
1478	        let vy = -8 + (k - 4.5);
1479	        vx *= 1.2; vy *= 1.2;
1480	        shoot(game, e.cx, e.cy, vx, vy, 40, 454, 600);
1481	      }
1482	      const t = smoothAmt((inSlot - 30) / 180);
1483	      handSeek(e, ax + swx * t, ay + swy * t, 20, 0.5); // :37381-37387
1484	    } else if (inSlot < 282) {
1485	      frameT = 0;
1486	      e.vx *= 0.9; e.vy *= 0.9;
1487	    } else if (inSlot < 287) {
1488	      frameT = 1;
1489	      e.vx *= 0.9; e.vy *= 0.9;
1490	    } else if (inSlot < 292) {
1491	      frameT = 2;
1492	      e.vx *= 0.9; e.vy *= 0.9;
1493	    } else if (inSlot < 300) {
1494	      frameT = 3;
1495	      // :37407-37427 原版在此把本手全部 454 弹转向玩家(12 速);本工程投射物无寻的,
1496	      // 近似为朝玩家补射 6 发 454(40 伤)
1497	      if (inSlot === 292 && player) {
1498	        for (let k = 0; k < 6; k++) {
1499	          const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
1500	          const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
1501	          const ang = Math.atan2(dy, dx) + (k - 2.5) * 0.18;
1502	          shoot(game, e.cx, e.cy, Math.cos(ang) * 12, Math.sin(ang) * 12, 40, 454, 600);
1503	        }
1504	      }
1505	      const t = 1 - (inSlot - 270) / 30;
1506	      handSeek(e, ax + swx * smoothAmt(t), ay + swy * smoothAmt(t), 14, 0.1); // :37428-37434
1507	    } else {
1508	      frameT = 3;
1509	      handApproach(e, ax, ay, 8, 0.2); // :37436-37446 归位
1510	    }
1511	  } else if (e.ai0 === 3 && player) { // 三连幻影矢(:37448-37472)
1512	    frameT = 0;
1513	    const pvx = (player as unknown as { vx: number }).vx ?? 0;
1514	    const pvy = (player as unknown as { vy: number }).vy ?? 0;
1515	    const dx = player.cx + pvx * 20 - e.cx, dy = player.cy + pvy * 20 - e.cy; // :37455 预判 20t
1516	    x.l0 = angleLerp(x.l0, Math.atan2(dy, dx), 0.5);
1517	    x.l1 = Math.min(1, x.l1 + 0.05);
1518	    if (inSlot === dur - 35) game.playSfxWav?.('NPC_Killed_6', 1, e.cx, e.cy);   // :37464(组4 死音库,终审修)
1519	    if (inSlot === dur - 14 || inSlot === dur - 7 || inSlot === dur) {
1520	      // :37466-37471 从眼球位置射 462(30 伤,8 速)
1521	      const exx = Math.cos(x.l0) * 30 * x.l1, eyy = Math.sin(x.l0) * 66 * x.l1;
1522	      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
1523	      shoot(game, e.cx + exx, e.cy + eyy, (dx / d) * 8, (dy / d) * 8, 30, 462, 600);
1524	    }
1525	  }
1526	
1527	  // 睁闭眼帧推进(:37493-37509):每 tick 向目标帧走 1,dontTakeDamage = frame>=21
1528	  const frameTarget = frameT * 7;
1529	  if (frameTarget > x.frame) x.frame += 1;
1530	  else if (frameTarget < x.frame) x.frame -= 1;
1531	  x.frame = clamp(x.frame, 0, 21);
1532	
1533	  // 位置钳制(:37473-37492):下一位置锁在核心侧上方盒内
1534	  let minX = core.cx + 220 * side + 110 * side, minY = core.cy - 60 - 150;
1535	  let maxX = minX + 370 * side, maxY = minY + 150;
1536	  if (minX > maxX) { const t = minX; minX = maxX; maxX = t; }
1537	  if (minY > maxY) { const t = minY; minY = maxY; maxY = t; }
1538	  const nx = clamp(e.cx + e.vx, minX, maxX);
1539	  const ny = clamp(e.cy + e.vy, minY, maxY);
1540	  e.x = nx - e.w / 2;
1541	  e.y = ny - e.h / 2;
1542	
1543	  // 无敌:闭眼(frame>=21)或残肢态;可受击时不写 iframes,保留 hurt() 的 8t 受击免疫
1544	  if (x.frame >= 21 || e.ai0 === -2) e.iframes = 2;
1545	  e.spin = x.l0; // 手部眼球角度(渲染朝向)
1546	}
1547	
1548	/** 手部悬停(:37266-37279 / :37289-37302):核心侧上方 (350*side, -100),6 速/0.3 步进+半程混合 */
1549	function handHover(e: Enemy, core: Enemy, side: number) {
1550	  const dx = core.cx + 350 * side - e.cx;
1551	  const dy = core.cy - 100 - e.cy;
1552	  const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
1553	  if (d > 20) flyLerp(e, (dx / d) * 6, (dy / d) * 6, 0.3);
1554	}
1555	/** SmoothStep 趋近(:37358-37367 / :37438-37445):velocity = SmoothStep(v, normalize(delta)*min(spd,|delta|), k) */
1556	function handApproach(e: Enemy, tx: number, ty: number, spd: number, k: number) {
1557	  const dx = tx - e.cx, dy = ty - e.cy;
1558	  const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
1559	  if (d < 0.01) return;
1560	  const m = Math.min(spd, d);
1561	  e.vx = vSmoothStep(e.vx, (dx / d) * m, k);
1562	  e.vy = vSmoothStep(e.vy, (dy / d) * m, k);
1563	}
1564	/** 扫射段追随(:37381-37387 / :37428-37434):Lerp(v, normalize(delta)*min(spd,|delta|), k) */
1565	function handSeek(e: Enemy, tx: number, ty: number, spd: number, k: number) {
1566	  const dx = tx - e.cx, dy = ty - e.cy;
1567	  const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
1568	  if (d < 0.01) return;
1569	  e.vx = lerp(e.vx, (dx / d) * Math.min(spd, d), k);
1570	  e.vy = lerp(e.vy, (dy / d) * Math.min(spd, d), k);
1571	}
1572	/** 眼球环射一发 452(:37326-37334):从眼球椭圆外沿射出,沿眼球朝向 8 速,30 伤 */
1573	function handFireEye(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, x: HandX, side: number) {
1574	  const exx = Math.cos(x.l0) * 30 * x.l1, eyy = Math.sin(x.l0) * 66 * x.l1;
1575	  const d = Math.hypot(exx, eyy) || 1;
1576	  const px = e.cx + (exx / d) * 72.46 * 0.4 - side; // |vector159|=√(30²+66²)≈72.46(:37330)
1577	  const py = e.cy + (eyy / d) * 72.46 * 0.4 + 3;
1578	  shoot(game, px, py, (exx / d) * 8, (eyy / d) * 8, 30, 452, 600);
1579	}

1581	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
1582	// 月亮领主 aiStyle 79 头(NPC 396,NPC.cs:37712-38128)
1583	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
1584	// 恒定悬停在核心正上方 400px(无自主移动);攻击表 MoonLordAttacksArray[0,2](:6874-6883):
1585	//   [矢180, 闭30, 吸血435, 矢180, 光375]。
1586	// 攻击:0 睁眼瞄准(无敌)/ 1 死亡之光 455(75 伤,:37902-37916)/ 2 月噬 456(0 伤,:37957-37973)
1587	//   / 3 三连幻影矢 462(30 伤,:37991-38015)。-2 闭眼残肢(80 接触伤)/ -3 核心死亡后永闭。
1588	// 无敌语义:dontTakeDamage = localAI[3]>=15(:37724)。
1589	
1590	const ML_HEAD_ATTACKS: ReadonlyArray<readonly [number, number]> = [
1591	  [3, 180], [0, 30], [2, 435], [3, 180], [1, 375],
1592	];
1593	
1594	export function moonLordHeadAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
1595	  // 类默认 ai0=-1120 会令 `ai0>=0` 攻击表门恒假 = 头部冻结(2026-08-13 ai0 清查;
1596	  // 现网靠父核 :1184 预置 0 挡住,此为入口防御)。放行运行态 -2/-3(闭眼残肢)
1597	  if (e.ai0 < -3) e.ai0 = 0;
1598	  const core = (game.enemies() as Enemy[]).find((n) => n.id === e.ai3 && n.vanillaId === 398);
1599	  if (!core || core.dead) { e.dead = true; return; } // :37714-37719
1600	  // checkDead(:78845-78857)同手:血尽 → ai0=-2 闭眼残肢 + 召真眼 400
1601	  if (e.hp <= 0 && e.ai0 !== -2 && e.ai0 !== -3) {
1602	    e.ai0 = -2; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; e.hp = e.maxHp; e.iframes = 2;
1603	    spawnTrueEye(game, e);
1604	  }
1605	
1606	  const x = headOf(e);
1607	  e.vx = 0; e.vy = 0; // :37725
1608	  e.x = core.cx - e.w / 2; // :37726 恒定在核心上方 400px
1609	  e.y = core.cy - 400 - e.h / 2;
1610	
1611	  // 攻击表推进(:37732-37762;ai0<0 不参与)
1612	  let inSlot = 0, dur = 0;
1613	  if (e.ai0 >= 0) {
1614	    e.ai1 += 1;
1615	    const s = attackSlot(e, ML_HEAD_ATTACKS);
1616	    e.ai0 = s.atk; // :37756 原版 ai[0] 即攻击号(亦为头部状态)
1617	    inSlot = s.inSlot; dur = s.dur;
1618	  }
1619	  let f2T = 0, f3T = 0; // num1262/num1263(帧目标 = f2T*7 / f3T*5)
1620	
1621	  if (e.ai0 === -3) { // 核心死亡后永闭(:37763-37781;由 -2 分支转入 :37784-37788)
1622	    e.def.damage = 0;
1623	    e.spin = lerp(e.spin, Math.PI / 12, 0.07);
1624	    if (e.ai1 >= 32) e.ai1 = 0;
1625	    if (x.f2 < 14) x.f2 += 1;

1627	    return;
1628	  }
1629	  if (e.ai0 === -2) { // 闭眼残肢(:37782-37844)
1630	    if (core.ai0 === 2) { e.ai0 = -3; return; } // :37784-37788 转入永闭(仅此 tick 跳帧推进)
1631	    e.def.damage = 80;
1632	    e.iframes = 2;
1633	    if (e.ai1 >= 32) e.ai1 = 0;
1634	    e.ai2 += 1;
1635	    if (e.ai2 >= 555) e.ai2 = 0;

1637	      const lt = e.ai2 - 120;

1640	      }
1641	      // :37828-37842 lt=120/180/240 于玩家处召 401 月噬怪(门禁说明见 spawnMoonLeech)
1642	      if ((lt === 120 || lt === 180 || lt === 240)) spawnMoonLeech(game, e, player);

1645	    // f2/f3 帧目标(0)永不收敛 = 残肢冻帧(2026-08-19 台账 E 修)
1646	  }
1647	
1648	  if (e.ai0 === 0 && player) { // 睁眼瞄准(:37845-37864)
1649	    f3T = 3;
1650	    const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy + 22 - e.cy;
1651	    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;

1654	    x.l2 = lerp(x.l2, 1, 0.2);
1655	  } else if (e.ai0 === 1 && player) { // 死亡之光(:37865-37952)
1656	    if (inSlot < 180) {
1657	      x.l1 = Math.max(0, x.l1 - 0.05);

1659	        game.spawnParticles(

1661	      }
1662	    } else if (inSlot < dur - 15) {
1663	      // :37902-37916 发射 455 死亡之光(75 伤→命中×2=150):起角 ∓60° 预偏,
1664	      // 束体以 ±2π/540 每 t 扫射 180t(束体自转见 MLDeathray)
1665	      if (inSlot === 180) {
1666	        e.ai2 = mlDeathray(game, e, 'head', player, 75 * 2); // :37913 扫射编码角初值

1681	      // 原文先钳 0 再掷,状态尾段仅 ~15t 且 l1 自 1 收束,实际近似每轮死亡之光一发;

1693	                (Math.floor(Math.random() * 1600) - 1599) * 0.01, 70, 1021);
1694	            }
1695	          }
1696	        }
1697	      }
1698	    }
1699	  } else if (e.ai0 === 2) { // 月噬(:37953-37990)
1700	    f2T = 2;
1701	    f3T = 3;
1702	    if (inSlot === 0 && player) { // :37958-37973 额前 216px 朝玩家月噬 456(0 伤,MoonLeechProj)

5993	  private empressArmFrames(e: Enemy): [number, number] {
5994	    const t = e.ai1;
5995	    let left = 0, right = 0;
5996	    switch (e.ai0) {
5997	      case 6: left = right = t < 6 ? 3 : t < 174 ? 4 : t < 180 ? 0 : 3; break;
5998	      case 0: left = right = t < 106 ? 1 : t < 110 ? 0 : 3; break;
5999	      case 2: case 11: left = t < 5 ? 3 : t < 65 ? 4 : 3; break;
6000	      case 5: right = t < 6 ? 3 : t < 54 ? 4 : 3; break;
6001	      case 4: case 10: left = right = t < 6 ? 3 : t < 54 ? 4 : 3; break;
6002	      case 8: case 9: {
6003	        const base = t < 10 ? 3 : t < 20 ? 4 : t < 30 ? 6 : 3;
6004	        left = right = base;
6005	        break;
6006	      }
6007	      case 7: {
6008	        const num10 = 60; const num11 = 0; const num12 = 5;
6009	        if (t < num11 + num12) left = 3;
6010	        else if (t < num11 + num10) left = 4;
6011	        else if (t < num11 + num10 + num12) { left = 4; right = 3; }
6012	        else if (t < num11 + 2 * num10) { left = 4; right = 4; }
6013	        else if (t < num11 + 3 * num10) { left = 5; right = 4; }
6014	        else if (t < num11 + 4 * num10) { left = 5; right = 5; }
6015	        else left = 5;
6016	        break;
6017	      }
6018	      default: break;
6019	    }
6020	    return [left, right];
6021	  }

// 弹跳球族增量（2026-08-11）：665 ChaosBallTim / 666 VileSpitEaterOfWorlds 两条取自
// Terarria1456/NPC.cs SetDefaults :17338-17367（aiStyle 9，字段对齐 30/112 同族；贴图
// NPC_665/666.png 已在 sprites，此前纯数据缺口）。
// 星璇塔增量（2026-08-11）：403/404（星尘蠕虫身/尾，字段复制 402 改宽高——1456 无独立
// SetDefaults，原版由 DrawNPC case 402 自绘 trail；本仓蠕虫链 spawnWormChain 需要
// 头+1/头+2 条目）、408（小水流入侵者，复制 407 缩小——1456 无独立 SetDefaults）。
// 城镇宠物声音增量（2026-08-18）：637/638/656 补 HitSound/DeathSound（1456
// SetDefaults :17142-17180/:17230-17244，提取器漏——637 猫 Hit1/Death6、
// 638 狗 Hit1/Death1、656 兔 Hit1/Death6；消费端 TownNPC.hurt/checkDead 数据驱动）。
// aiStyle 与 AI_XXX 方法的对应见 NPC.cs AI() 分发；此处先覆盖高频家族，
// 未移植 AI 家族的 aiStyle 回退到最接近的已移植行为（注释标注）。
import vanillaNpcsJson from '../../public/sprites/vanilla-npcs.json';
import vanillaDataJson from '../../public/sprites/vanilla.json';
import soundTracksJson from './vanilla-soundtracks.json';
import { ITEM_BY_KEY } from './items';
import { TILE_DEFS } from './tiles';

/** tile id → key 反查（biomeAt 群系判定用） */
const TILE_KEY_NAME: string[] = TILE_DEFS.map((d) => d.key);

// （旧掉落表已退役：掉落走 src/drops/NpcDrops.ts 规则树——
//  tools/extract-npcdrops.mjs 结构化提取 ItemDropDatabase.cs，含专家/大师/条件/链语义）

/** 原版物品 id → 本仓库 item key（vanilla.json 的 key 是 PascalCase，ITEM_BY_KEY 多为 snake_case；
 *  未注册的返回 null 跳过） */
export const vanillaItemKey = (() => {
  const map = new Map<number, string | null>();
  const items = (vanillaDataJson as unknown as { items: Record<string, { key?: string }> }).items ?? {};
  return (itemId: number): string | null => {
    if (map.has(itemId)) return map.get(itemId)!;
    const meta = items[String(itemId)];
    let key: string | null = null;
    if (meta?.key) {
      const snake = meta.key.replace(/([a-z0-9])([A-Z])/g, '$1_$2').toLowerCase();
      if (ITEM_BY_KEY[snake] != null) key = snake;
      else if (ITEM_BY_KEY[meta.key] != null) key = meta.key;
      // vi_NNN 导入物品（items.ts 的全量原版物品命名）：vi_<id> 或 vi_<id>_<snake>
      else if (ITEM_BY_KEY[`vi_${itemId}`] != null) key = `vi_${itemId}`;
      else if (ITEM_BY_KEY[`vi_${itemId}_${snake}`] != null) key = `vi_${itemId}_${snake}`;
    }
    map.set(itemId, key);
    return key;
  };
})();

export interface VanillaNpc {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  frames: number;
  lifeMax: number;
  damage: number;
  defense: number;
  knockBackResist: number;
  aiStyle: number;
  width: number;
  height: number;
  npcSlots: number;

Notes:
- Extra_21 is 36×96 but slices are 16px tall with wrap when Y+16 > 96 (i.e., rows advance 16*row,16*row+16,... wrap to 0 when exceeding 96 — so rows 0..5 then wrap; effectively arbitrary offset start from timeLeft/3%5).
- Origin for beam segments: (width/2, 0) — top-center anchored, drawing downward along rotation direction. Velocity direction is along rotation. In our canvas: translate to center3, rotate(angle), drawImage(ex21, sx=row-based, 0, w, 16, -w/2, 0, w, 16).
- The muzzle (Projectile_455 36×14) at center, origin size/2, scale.
- End cap Extra_22 (36×20) with origin value72.Frame().Top() = (0,0), i.e., top-left corner at center3, rotated.
- Alpha: color (255,255,255,0)*0.9 → effectively additive blending white with alpha 0? EntitySpriteDraw likely uses additive... Hmm, in XNA spritebatch with default state alpha 0 = invisible; but "Color(255,255,255,0) * 0.9" multiplied: (229,229,229,0). Alpha=0 would make it invisible unless the blend mode is additive. Terraria draws lasers with... hmm, actually in Terraria, `new Color(255,255,255,0)` with alpha 0 in non-premultiplied... In XNA Color * float multiplies all channels including A: A = 0*0.9 = 0. Yet it's visible — so the sprite batch at that point must be in Additive blend mode? Let me check the context — this section might be within `spriteBatch.Begin(... BlendState.Additive)`? Those are unrelated (melee weapon draws). So the 455 draw is in the main projectile pass. But wait — is the deathray drawn in the main `DrawProjectiles` loop or in a special "draw before NPCs" pass? Also `EntitySpriteDraw` — let me check what that is. And the alpha-0 color: in XNA non-premultiplied AlphaBlend, alpha 0 would be invisible... but hold on — XNA's SpriteBatch default blend uses non-premultiplied? Terraria uses `BlendState.AlphaBlend` which is alpha-premultiplied blending: result = src + dst*(1-src.alpha) with source blend = One. With premultiplied blending (ColorSourceBlend = One), the source color (229,229,229,0) contributes RGB even with A=0! That's why `new Color(255,255,255,0)` renders as additive white glow. So effectively it's an additive-ish white glow: rgb 229 (0.9) added regardless of alpha.

So in canvas terms: use `globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter'` (or just draw with white). Our renderer draws with standard source-over; the vanilla effect is additive white at 0.9. I'll approximate with 'lighter' composite at alpha 0.9 — visually 1:1.

Also `EntitySpriteDraw` — the helper that applies gravityDir etc. Fine.

Now — where's `proj.scale` and `localAI[1]` (beam length) set: our MLDeathray already computes `scale` and `len` (localAI[1] = len). Good — the draw should use the same values. `proj.rotation` = angle. `proj.timeLeft` for Extra_21 row = timeLeft/3 % 5. Our MLDeathray has age (0..180) and 180-age remaining = timeLeft. Actually timeLeft starts at 180 and decrements; our age increments to 180. So timeLeft = 180 - age. Row = floor((180-age)/3) % 5.

Wait — check Projectile.cs 455: timeLeft initial value? Let me check the AI (30291-30549) for timeLeft handling — maybe timeLeft gets set to 180 at fire. Our implementation treats lifetime 180. Let me look at Projectile.cs quickly for 455's timeLeft and light emission (CastLight / DelegateMethods.castLight).

The `lightRGB` self-report mechanism: any entity in projectiles bucket with `lightRGB: [r,g,b]` gets a point light at its center. For the deathray, the vanilla light is a line (PlotTileLine from Center to Center+velocity·len, width·scale, color (0.3,0.65,0.7)). Approximating with a point light at the center would be poor for a 2400px beam. Better: in MLDeathray.fixedUpdate (or the light pass), plot the light along the beam line. Since the lightRGB channel only supports one point with optional lightRGBAt offset, I could instead directly use `game.lighting`? Does GameHooks expose lighting? Let me check types.ts GameHooks for lighting access.

Actually — simpler: the entity-light pass reads `lightRGB` from entities in buckets. MLDeathray is in projectiles bucket. I can make `lightRGB` a getter returning the color, but the position is just one point. For a beam, I could override... Hmm, look at how the game handles the last prism (632/633 PrismProj) — line light? PrismProj has lightRGB [0.5,0.2,0.5] point light. So point approximation is the existing precedent for beams. But we can do better cheaply: since PlotTileLine plots along the line, I can plot several points along the beam via lightRGBAt? No — single.

Alternative: check whether GameHooks gives access to `lighting`. Let me look at types.ts.

  static newGore(
    type: number, x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number, scale = 1, perfect = false,

    if (type < 0 || livePool.size >= MAX_GORE) return null;
    const g = new GorePiece(type, x, y, scale);
    g.vx = vx; g.vy = vy;
    if (!perfect) {
      // :1347-1348 随机扰动：vy -= Next(10,31)*0.1 ∈ [-3.0,-1.0]；vx += Next(-20,21)*0.1 ∈ [-2.0,2.0]

      g.vx += irand2(-20, 21) * 0.1;
    }
    g.rotation = 0;
    g.alpha = 0;
    // :1339-1345 ChildSafety：童模开启 → 危险 gore 替换 11-13（scale 半随机、速度减半），
    // 替换先于粘滞/时档/16/17/SpecialAI 分支（后续判定均按新 type）
    let t = type;
    if (!ChildSafety.disabled && ChildSafety.dangerous(t)) {
      t = 11 + irand(3);
      g.goreIdCompat(t);
      g.scale = Math.random() * 0.5 + 0.5;
      g.vx /= 2;
      g.vy /= 2;
      g.resizeBox();
    }
    // :1361-1374 粘滞与时档
    if (NON_STICKY_TYPES.has(t)) {
      g.sticky = false;
    } else if (t >= 375 && t <= 377) {
      g.sticky = false;
      g.alpha = 100;
    } else {
      g.sticky = true;
      g.timeLeft = GORE_TIME;
    }
    // :1487-1490 16/17 火苗族：alpha=100 + scale=0.7 + light=1
    if (t === 16 || t === 17) {
      g.alpha = 100;
      g.scale = 0.7;
      g.light = 1;
      g.resizeBox();
    }

1047	/** 图标矩形乘色（离屏三步:draw → multiply → destination-in 恢复 alpha）。

1049	const tintScratch = typeof document !== 'undefined' ? document.createElement('canvas') : null;
1050	function tintIconRect(
1051	  ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D,
1052	  icon: { img: CanvasImageSource; sx: number; sy: number; sw: number; sh: number },
1053	  dst: { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number },
1054	  tint: readonly [number, number, number],
1055	): void {
1056	  if (!tintScratch) return;
1057	  if (tintScratch.width !== dst.w || tintScratch.height !== dst.h) {
1058	    tintScratch.width = dst.w;
1059	    tintScratch.height = dst.h;
1060	  }
1061	  const octx = tintScratch.getContext('2d');
1062	  if (!octx) return;
1063	  octx.clearRect(0, 0, dst.w, dst.h);
1064	  octx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
1065	  octx.drawImage(icon.img, icon.sx, icon.sy, icon.sw, icon.sh, 0, 0, dst.w, dst.h);
1066	  octx.globalCompositeOperation = 'multiply';
1067	  octx.fillStyle = `rgb(${tint[0] | 0},${tint[1] | 0},${tint[2] | 0})`;
1068	  octx.fillRect(0, 0, dst.w, dst.h);
1069	  octx.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-in';
1070	  octx.drawImage(icon.img, icon.sx, icon.sy, icon.sw, icon.sh, 0, 0, dst.w, dst.h);
1071	  octx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
1072	  ctx.drawImage(tintScratch, dst.x, dst.y);
1073	}

201:export function spawnPart(game: GameHooks, src: Enemy, id: number): Enemy | null {
864:export function accelTo(v: number, target: number, a: number): number {
879:export function losClear(game: GameHooks, x0: number, y0: number, x1: number, y1: number): boolean {
885:export function shoot(game: GameHooks, x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number, dmg: number, projId: number, life = 300, arrowOpts?: Partial<import('./Arrow').ArrowOpts>): void {
export function shoot(game: GameHooks, x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number, dmg: number, projId: number, life = 300, arrowOpts?: Partial<import('./Arrow').ArrowOpts>): void {
  (game as unknown as { entities: { add: (e: unknown, b: string) => void } }).entities.add(
    new Arrow(x, y, vx, vy, dmg, 0, projId, null, { grav: 0, life, pierce: 1, hostile: true, ...arrowOpts }), 'projectiles');
}

/** NPC.SpawnMechQueen 合体召唤链(NPC.cs:19739-19748,Game.summonMechdusa 调用)：
 *  queen(127) 落位 → markMechQueen(=SpawnBoss :81472 写 ai[3]=mechQueen 非零标记)
 *  → 以 queen Center 为共同出生点同点 NewNPC 125/126/134 + 2×139 探针
 *  (ai2=毁灭者头引用,ai3=∓1 侧位——原版第 7/8 参 ai2/ai3,曾误记 ai0)。
 *  调用方须已过 anyMechBossUp 门与 Mechdusa 种子门(remix+getGood)。 */
export function spawnMechQueenEnsemble(game: GameHooks, cx: number, cy: number): Enemy | null {
  const queen = spawnVanillaEnemy(game, 127, cx, cy);
  if (!queen) return null;
  // SpawnBoss :81469-81473：Type==127 且 mechQueen!=-1 → ai[3]=whoAmI(非零标记)；
  // 本仓 ai3 是旋冲方向缓存(±1)不冲突——mech 态旋转走 AngleLerp 不读 ai3,标记走 WeakSet
  markMechQueen(queen);
  queen.ai3 = queen.id;
  // :19743-19747 同点伴随(取 mechQueen 的 Center);探针 ai2=134 引用、ai3=∓1 侧位
  for (const id of [125, 126]) spawnVanillaEnemy(game, id, queen.cx, queen.cy);
  const head = spawnVanillaEnemy(game, 134, queen.cx, queen.cy);
  for (const side of [-1, 1]) {
    const probe = spawnVanillaEnemy(game, 139, queen.cx, queen.cy);
    if (probe) { probe.ai2 = head ? head.id : -1; probe.ai3 = side; }
  }
  return queen;
}

18:export function projSprite(projId: number): ImageBitmap | HTMLImageElement | null {
48:export function projFrameCount(projId: number): number {
61:export function projFrameImg(projId: number, frameIdx: number): HTMLCanvasElement | null {
export function projSprite(projId: number): ImageBitmap | HTMLImageElement | null {
  let img = spriteCache.get(projId);
  if (img !== undefined) return img ?? null;
  if (typeof Image === 'undefined') return null;
  const im = new Image();
  im.onload = () => upgradeToBitmap(im, (b) => spriteCache.set(projId, b));
  img = im;
  img.src = `sprites/vanilla/Projectile_${projId}.png`;
  spriteCache.set(projId, img);
  return img;
}
