// 后期 Boss AI 批探针(bossAI_lategame.ts 邪教徒 439/克隆体 440/女皇 636/史后 657
// + bossAI_duke_moonlord.ts 月总召怪段):
//   克隆体生成节拍 / 环位补位 / 真假区分与死亡反馈 / 远古之光 522 轨道弹 /
//   史后仆从 658-660 fromVanilla / 弹幕贴图管线 sprite 断言 / 月总真眼与月噬怪召出。
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { Enemy } from '../src/entities/Enemy';
import { World } from '../src/world/World';
import { TILE } from '../src/core/constants';
import { spawnPart } from '../src/entities/bossAI';
import { BuffState, BuffType } from '../src/stats/Buffs';
import type { GameHooks } from '../src/entities/types';

const W = 160, H = 120, FLOOR = 100;

function makeWorld(): World {
  const w = new World(W, H, 7, 'lategame-test');
  const st = w.store;
  w.groundLevel = 50;
  for (let x = 0; x < W; x++) {
    st.setTile(x, FLOOR, 1);
    st.setTile(x, FLOOR + 1, 1);
  }
  return w;
}

function makeHooks(world: World, playerAt: { x: number; y: number }) {
  const enemies: Enemy[] = [];
  const projectiles: unknown[] = [];
  const em = {
    nextId: 1,
    add: (e: unknown, bucket: 'enemies' | 'projectiles') => {
      const ent = e as { id: number };
      ent.id = em.nextId++;
      if (bucket === 'enemies') enemies.push(e as Enemy);
      else projectiles.push(e);
    },
  };
  const px = playerAt.x * TILE, py = playerAt.y * TILE;
  const player = {
    x: px, y: py, w: 20, h: 42, cx: px + 10, cy: py + 21,
    vx: 0, vy: 0, hp: 400, maxHp: 400, dead: false, useTime: 1,
    buffs: new BuffState(),
    aabbOverlaps: () => false,
  };
  const hooks = {
    world, player,
    enemies: () => enemies,
    critters: () => [],
    spawnDrop: () => null,
    damagePlayer: () => {},
    addDamageNumber: () => {},
    cutTile: () => {},
    onEnemyKilled: () => {},
    spawnEnemy: () => {},
    spawnParticles: () => {},
    notifyInventoryChanged: () => {},
    playSfx: () => {},
    playSfxFiles: () => {},
    showPickupLabel: () => {},
    entities: em,
  } as unknown as GameHooks;
  return { hooks, enemies, projectiles, em, player };
}

function spawn(h: ReturnType<typeof makeHooks>, id: number, tx: number, ty: number): Enemy {
  const e = Enemy.fromVanilla(id, tx * TILE, ty * TILE)!;
  e.id = h.em.nextId++;
  h.enemies.push(e);
  return e;
}

/** 逐 tick 驱动(默认全部敌人——克隆体镜像需要与真身同拍推进) */
function tick(h: ReturnType<typeof makeHooks>, n: number, only?: Enemy[]) {
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    for (const e of only ?? h.enemies) {
      if (!e.dead) e.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, h.hooks);
    }
  }
}

```

    ctx.fillStyle = '#F0C8FF';
    ctx.fillRect(0, -thick / 4, this.len, thick / 2);
    ctx.restore();
  }

```ts
  draw(r: Renderer, _cam: Camera): void {
    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');
    if (!ctx) return;
    const scale = Math.min(this.maxScale, Math.sin((this.age * Math.PI) / 180) * 10 * this.maxScale);
    if (scale <= 0.01 || this.len <= 1) return;
    // 原版三件套(Main.cs:31951-31992):炮口 Projectile_455(origin 尺寸/2)→
    // 束身 Extra_21 五帧行(timeLeft/3%5 起,16px 段沿束向平铺,origin (w/2,0),
    // 段行 +16 滚动、越 96 回 0,尾段按余量截高)→ 端帽 Extra_22(origin (0,0));
    // 颜色 (255,255,255,0)×0.9 = XNA 预乘混色下的加性白 → canvas lighter 近似。
    // 绘制角 = 束角 − π/2(Projectile.cs:30483 rotation = num731 − π/2,纹理竖排)。
    const muzzle = projSprite(455);
    const ex21 = r.atlas?.ensureVImage?.('vanilla/Extra_21.png') ?? null;
    const ex22 = r.atlas?.ensureVImage?.('vanilla/Extra_22.png') ?? null;
    if (!muzzle || muzzle.width === 0 || !ex21 || !ex22 || ex21.width === 0) {
      // 贴图未就绪兜底(首帧):旧自绘紫矩形
      ...
      return;
    }
    ctx.save();
    ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter';
    ctx.globalAlpha = 0.9;
    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
    ctx.translate(this.cx, this.cy);
    ctx.rotate(this.angle - Math.PI / 2);
    // 炮口(:31961)
    ctx.drawImage(muzzle, -muzzle.width / 2, -muzzle.height / 2, muzzle.width * scale, muzzle.height * scale);
    // 束身:有效长 = len −(炮口半高 + 端帽高)·scale;自炮口前沿沿束向 16px·scale 平铺
    let remain = this.len - (muzzle.height / 2 + ex22.height) * scale;
    let adv = 0;
    let sy = 16 * (Math.floor(((600 - this.age) / 3)) % 5);   // timeLeft=600−age
    let cx0 = muzzle.height / 2 * scale;    // 束身起点(沿束向,局部 +y)
    while (adv + 1 < remain) {
      let h = 16;
      if (remain - adv < h) h = remain - adv;
      ctx.drawImage(ex21, 0, sy, ex21.width, h, -ex21.width / 2, cx0 + adv, ex21.width * scale, h * scale);
      adv += h * scale;
      sy += 16;
      if (sy + 16 > ex21.height) sy = 0;   // wait — vanilla checks value73.Y + value73.Height > height
    }
    // 端帽(:31988,origin = (0,0))
    const capY = muzzle.height / 2 * scale + adv;   // = center3 offset
    ctx.drawImage(ex22, 0, 0, ex22.width, ex22.height, 0, capY, ex22.width * scale, ex22.height * scale);
    ctx.restore();
  }
```

Segment draw origin (w/2, 0) → drawn from center3 extending +y (in rotated frame). ✓ `num279 += value73.Height * proj.scale` — advanced distance in world px; my `adv += h * scale` ✓ (h in texture px).

With full 16 height: sy+16 > 96 → sy > 80 → wraps at sy=96 → 0... wait sy increments by 16: 0,16,...,80,96 → 96+16=112 > 96 → 0. ```
  const im = new Image();
  im.onload = () => upgradeToBitmap(im, (b) => spriteCache.set(projId, b));
  img = im;
  img.src = `sprites/vanilla/Projectile_${projId}.png`;
  spriteCache.set(projId, img);
  return img;
}

/** 预热弹幕贴图(2026-08-13,用户报"发射阶段回退兜底,过一会才正确"):
 *  懒加载下首发射击时表未就绪会先画短线兜底。进图/背包变化时把武器/弹药的
 *  shoot 链先 kick 掉(占位即触发加载,缓存 Map 去重),发射时已就绪 */
export function prefetchProjectiles(ids: Iterable<number>): void {
  if (typeof Image === 'undefined') return;
  for (const id of ids) {
    if (!Number.isInteger(id) || id <= 0) continue;
    projSprite(id);
  }
}

/** Main.projFrames（Main.cs:8392 起全 275 条非 1 帧赋值，tools 内联提取）：
 *  未列入的恒 1 帧。投射物贴图是【竖向多帧行】——帧高 = 图高/帧数，
 *  整图绘制会把多帧压成胶片条（史莱姆随从 266 曾 12 帧压成一坨） */
import projFramesJson from '../data/vanilla-projframes.json';
import { projectileData } from '../data/vanillaProjectiles';
import { projGravSpec } from '../data/vanillaItemCombat';
const PROJ_FRAMES = projFramesJson as Record<string, number>;
export function projFrameCount(projId: number): number {
  return PROJ_FRAMES[String(projId)] ?? 1;
}

/** 单帧裁切缓存（id+帧号 → canvas），多帧行按帧高切片 */
const frameCache = new Map<string, HTMLCanvasElement>();
/** 热补丁替换弹幕贴图(2026-08-19 素材重制):写 spriteCache + 清该 id 的
 *  frameCache 条目(键 `id|idx` 不含 texId,不自动失效)。RemasterRuntime 调用。 */
export function setProjSpriteOverride(projId: number, img: ImageBitmap | HTMLImageElement): void {
  spriteCache.set(projId, img);
  const prefix = `${projId}|`;
  for (const k of frameCache.keys()) if (k.startsWith(prefix)) frameCache.delete(k);
}

 "new_string": "  draw(r: Renderer, _cam: Camera): void {\n    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');\n    if (!ctx) return;\n    const scale = Math.min(this.maxScale, Math.sin((this.age * Math.PI) / 180) * 10 * this.maxScale);\n    if (scale <= 0.01 || this.len <= 1) return;\n    // 原版三件套(Main.cs:31951-31992):炮口 Projectile_455(origin 尺寸/2)→\n    // 束身 Extra_21 五帧行平铺(行 = timeLeft/3%5 起、16px 段沿束向逐段 +16 滚动、\n    // 越 96 回 0、尾段按余量截高,origin (w/2,0))→ 端帽 Extra_22(origin (0,0))。\n    // 颜色 (255,255,255,0)×0.9 = XNA 预乘混色下的加性白光 → canvas lighter 近似;\n    // 绘制角 = 束角 − π/2(Projectile.cs:30483 rotation = num731 − π/2,纹理竖排)。\n    const muzzle = projSprite(455);\n    const ex21 = r.atlas?.ensureVImage?.('vanilla/Extra_21.png') ?? null;\n    const ex22 = r.atlas?.ensureVImage?.('vanilla/Extra_22.png') ?? null;\n    if (!muzzle || muzzle.width === 0 || !ex21 || !ex22 || ex21.width === 0 || ex22.width === 0) {\n      // 贴图未就绪兜底(懒加载首帧):旧自绘紫矩形,加载完成后自愈\n      const thick = Math.max(2, 36 * scale);\n      ctx.save();\n      ctx.globalAlpha = clamp(scale * 1.6, 0.25, 1);\n      ctx.translate(this.cx, this.cy);\n      ctx.rotate(this.angle);\n      ctx.fillStyle = '#B040D0';\n      ctx.fillRect(0, -thick / 2, this.len, thick);\n      ctx.restore();\n      return;\n    }\n    ctx.save();\n    ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter';\n    ctx.globalAlpha = 0.9;\n    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;\n    ctx.translate(this.cx, this.cy);\n    ctx.rotate(this.angle - Math.PI / 2);\n    // 炮口(:31961)\n    ctx.drawImage(muzzle, -muzzle.width / 2, -muzzle.height / 2,\n      muzzle.width * scale, muzzle.height * scale);\n    // 束身(:31966-31986):有效长 = len −(炮口半高 + 端帽高)·scale,自炮口前沿\n    // (局部 +y = 束向)逐段 16px·scale 平铺\n    const remain = this.len - (muzzle.height / 2 + ex22.height) * scale;\n    let adv = 0;\n    let sy = 16 * (Math.floor((600 - this.age) / 3) % 5);   // 行 = timeLeft/3%5\n    const startY = (muzzle.height / 2) * scale;\n    while (adv + 1 < remain) {\n      let h = 16;\n      if (remain - adv < h) h = remain - adv;\n      ctx.drawImage(ex21, 0, sy, ex21.width, h, -ex21.width / 2, startY + adv,\n        ex21.width * scale, h * scale);\n      adv += h * scale;\n      sy += 16;\n      if (sy + 16 > ex21.height) sy = 0;                    // 段行滚动回卷\n    }\n    // 端帽(:31988,origin = (0,0))\n    ctx.drawImage(ex22, 0, 0, ex22.width, ex22.height, 0, startY + adv,\n      ex22.width * scale, ex22.height * scale);\n    ctx.restore();\n  }\n}"

mySpriteBatch.Draw(value35, rCurrentNPC.Center - screenPos + vector33 + vector30, null, alpha6, rCurrentNPC.rotation, new Vector2(value35.Width, value35.Height) / 2f, 1f, SpriteEffects.None, 0f);
```
So the pupil is also rotated by npc.rotation. With rot normally 0, no-op. Apply for completeness.

Also update the docstring of drawMoonLordHead (lines 5855-5858).

function mlClearDeathrayProjectiles(game: GameHooks): void {
  const ents = (game as unknown as {
    entities?: { projectiles?: Array<{ dead?: boolean; projId?: number }> };
  }).entities;
  for (const p of ents?.projectiles ?? []) {
    if (p.dead) continue;
    const id = p.projId;
    if (id === 456 || id === 462 || id === 455 || id === 452 || id === 454) p.dead = true;
  }
  for (const n of game.enemies() as Enemy[]) {
    if (!n.dead && n.vanillaId === 400) n.dead = true;
  }
}

- 手 ：1655-1657：每帧尾部 `e.lai0=x.l0; e.lai1=x.l1; e.customFrameIdx=floor(x.frame/7)`（FindFrame case 397 `:72644-72648` frame.Y=num·frameCounter/7，开合帧 0/7/14/21 由攻击态推进）——Renderer:5834 瞳孔与 :5843 手本体帧全部活起来。
- 头 ：1860-1866：`lai0=x.l0 / lai1=x.l1 / lai2=x.f2 / lai3=x.f3`（Main.cs:24464 椭圆 (27,59)×localAI[1]、:24468-24475 Extra_25 行=localAI[2]/7、Extra_29 行=localAI[3]/5；f2/f3↔localAI[2]/[3] 已对 vanilla :38016-38049 核验）。
- 头 3×3 帧：Renderer.ts:1147 `vanillaFrameIdx` 加 case 396 = `floor(t/4)%8`（8 帧@4t，跳过第 9 帧；10% 抖动不可复现已登记）；drawMoonLordHead :6087-6088 改用 vanillaFrameIdx(e,8) 并修正 col/row 映射为 vanilla `Frame(3,3, frame.Y/3, frame.Y%3)` 的 col=idx/3、row=idx%3（原 col/row 反了）。另头各层加 rotation（仅 -3 永闭态 e.spin 承载 ：37767，活态恒 0——e.spin 另载瞳角是既有约定，测试有断言故未动）。
- 核心帧：bossAI :1433-1455（WeakMap CoreX）：ai0<=0 恒帧 0；ai0>0 时 fc+1、≥30 回卷 6、帧=floor(fc%30/6)（帧 0 仅首程 5t）；**睁核瞬间一次性** gore 619-622（GorePiece.newGore，position+(∓10,∓15)）+ 尘 209×50 爆品红（哨兵 burst 标记），帧号写 e.customFrameIdx 供 drawMoonLordCore 直读。

**【二、游离眼 400 专属渲染】**
- bossAI_lunar_misc.ts:315-322：moonLordFreeEyeAI 尾部镜像 `lai0=x[0](瞳角)/lai1=x[1](睁眼)/lai2=x[2](缩放)`；rotation 复用 AI 已写的 e.spin（原版仅攻击 2/3 写 rotation 余态保值，已核 ：38480/:38505/:38553/:38565），未动 vanillaScale 乘区。
- Renderer.ts:4297 分派 + :6130-6160 新 `drawMoonLordFreeEye`（Main.cs:24492-24504 1:1）：本体 vnpc(400, vanillaFrameIdx)（:1150 新 case 400 = 4t/帧、ai0∈{0,3} 时 2t，:72650-72664）、origin(40,40)、随 e.spin 旋转；瞳孔 Extra_19 = 椭圆(30,30)×lai1 @lai0、origin 尺寸/2、整体 scale=lai2（出生 0 渐入，与原版一致）、随 rotation、不镜像。

**【三、455 死光绘制/光照 1:1】** bossAI :1186-1230 draw 重写 + :1128-1153 AI 侧补
- 绘制三件套（Main.cs:31951-31992）：炮口 Projectile_455(origin 尺寸/2)→束身 Extra_21 五帧行平铺（行=timeLeft/3%5=`floor((600−age)/3)%5` 起、16px 段沿束向逐段 +16 滚动、越 96 回 0、尾段截高、origin(w/2,0)）→端帽 Extra_22(origin(0,0))；颜色 (255,255,255,0)×0.9=XNA 预乘加性白 → canvas `lighter` 近似；绘制角=束角−π/2（:30483）。贴图未就绪保留旧紫矩形兜底自愈。锚点 'core' 档同步改读 400 的 lai0/lai1（ellipse(30,30)，与束角解码步进锁步 ：38656-38663）。
- 双尘 229(Vortex 青焰)×2 @束端 Center+dir·(len−14) ±π/2 侧抛 2-4 速 + 1/5 尘 31 烟束（偏移幅=width 36 非 ×scale，:30524-30540）；束光 `game.addLightAt` 沿 PlotTileLine 三线带（中心±width·scale/2、逐 16px）投 **(0.3, 0.65, 0.7)**（:30541-30543）。

**【四、核心演出 622 星屑】** bossAI :1378-1392（NPC.cs:37022-37041）：死亡态每 15t（90≤ai1<480）周身 20-420px 随机**非实心**格掷 proj 622（速度 =(0,−rand·0.5−0.5).RotatedBy(±π/8..3π/8)×6，extraUpdates2=3 步/t → Arrow 直飞 ×3 折算、寿命 23t、dmg 0 非 hostile，视觉对齐）。

**【五、出生/传送音时机】** bossAI :1309 与 :1315：-1 演出 Zombie_92 移到 `ai1===30`（:36816，原误在 ≥60 段尾）；-2 传送归位段补 `ai1===30` 同款（:36792-36795，原整段无声）。
